CLI AFTERLIFE TURN EXAMPLES — CHAOS SEA AND SHINING ABODE

This file supplements:
- CLI_Agent_Daemon_Specification.md
- TaskGuides/CLI_Step_Main.txt
- Rules/Block_0.txt
- Rules/Block_CLI_Operations.txt
- CLI_API_Specification.md
- OtherGuides/Afterlife_Contract_Matrix.md

Read this file whenever `game_state/meta/soul_state.json.currentRealm` is `Chaos Sea`, `Море Хаоса`, `Shining Abode`, or `Сияющая Обитель`.

Before using an example, first select the matching rows in `OtherGuides/Afterlife_Contract_Matrix.md`. The matrix tells the GM which scheduler contour, pending file, direct action, canonical output surface, receipt/report, and forbidden substitution apply. These examples then show worked accepted-turn patterns. They are not a replacement for the full schemas.

==================================================
EXAMPLE INDEX
==================================================

Use this index before resolving an afterlife turn:

- Contract routing for all rows below: read `OtherGuides/Afterlife_Contract_Matrix.md` first.
- Scheduler / catch-up / resident history in Chaos Sea: see example 1.
- Active Shining core action + faction/trade/resident living world: see example 2.
- Shining pending-bootstrap `TriggerIncarnation`: see example 3.
- `pending_guardian_trade_request.json`: see example 4.
- `pending_archive_consultation_request.json` / `pending_archive_project_fuel_request.json`: see example 5.
- Ordinary Chaos Sea `TriggerIncarnation`: see example 6.
- Direct Chaos Sea `/gacha` and guardian-mediated gacha: see example 7.
- `[INK_FEATHER_ACTION: ABODE_OFFERING]` for `offeringType = ink_feathers` only: see example 8 and `Examples/E_CLI_Ink_Feather_Actions.txt`.
- Shining faction founding / realignment / leadership pending files: see example 9.
- `pending_guardian_abode_residents_request.json`: see example 10.
- `pending_guardian_abode_resident_transfers.json`: see example 11.
- `pending_guardian_social_interactions.json`: see example 12.
- `pending_player_guardian_foundation.json`: see example 13.
- All `pending_shining_abode_actions.json` `requests[].actionType` variants: see example 14.
- Freeform afterlife `UpdateGuardians` commands outside strict pending files: see example 15.
- Combined Chaos Sea scheduler + pending contract + player action: see example 16.
- Combined active Shining scheduler + core action + trade/resident consequence: see example 17.
- `prepare_incarnation_package` vs later Shining bootstrap handoff: see example 18.
- Ordinary Chaos Sea living world without pending files: see example 19.
- `system_guardian_attraction.json` / deterministic Eternal Guardian attraction: see example 20.
- `afterlife_return_guard.json` protected first afterlife turn: see example 21.
- Direct resident action tags and hidden pending-backed routing tags: see example 22.
- Freeform Chaos Sea Abode search from `/обители`: see example 23.
- Source of Light capstone (`/source_of_light` / `/источник_света`): see example 25.
- Player-facing Chaos Sea audit previews and notification receipt detail surfaces: see the audit checklist after example 23.

==================================================
GLOBAL AFTERLIFE TURN RULE
==================================================

Every accepted afterlife turn must follow this order:

1. Realm gate:
   - Read `game_state/meta/soul_state.json.currentRealm`.
   - Decide whether this is ordinary Chaos Sea, ordinary active Shining Abode, or Shining Abode pending-bootstrap handoff.

2. Lifecycle guards:
   - Check `afterlife_return_guard.json`, `ascension.json`, `incarnation_trigger.json`, and `shining_abode_state.json.preparedIncarnationPackage`.
   - Do not mix return, life evaluation, ascension, incarnation, and Shining bootstrap logic.

3. Scheduler first:
   - Read `input/turn_request.json.progressionControl` before resolving the player action.
   - Every `mustEvaluate* = true` is mandatory.
   - `afterlifeCatchupRequired = true` is one bounded summary obligation, not thousands of raw cycles.
   - `game_state/control/progression_schedule.json` is client-owned; never edit it directly. Close scheduler obligations only through `progressionProcessingReport` / `game_state/control/progression_report.json`.

4. State loading:
   - Read `soul_state.json`.
   - Read `guardians.json`.
   - Read `guardian_projects.json`.
   - Read `guardian_abode_residents.json`.
   - In Shining Abode, also read `shining_abode_state.json`.
   - If `game_state/control/next_life_scenario_core.json` exists, read it as client-owned next-life bootstrap context. Do not edit or close it. `scenarioCoreAssertions[]` are hard facts for the next life; `candidateAssertions[]` are candidate-only until later accepted state confirms them.

5. Pending contracts:
   - Read pending afterlife request files in `game_state/control/`.
   - Close every present pending contract through its canonical receipt/state surface.

6. Actor scope:
   - Declare every changed Guardian, resident, Shining faction, hall, gate, or institution as relevant.
   - Explain why plausible non-processed actors are outside scope.

7. Living-world debt:
   - Process due contours through Guardian/Soul/resident/Shining surfaces.
   - Do not use Mortal World NPC/faction/location/time/weather/combat/inventory channels.

8. Direct player action:
   - Process the player action only after the above context is known.

9. Report:
   - If any afterlife cycle is due, write `progressionProcessingReport` with exact processed counts and exact per-contour `newLast*Ordinal`.
   - Copy `sessionId`, `requestId`, and `turnNumber` from the current `input/turn_request.json`; do not invent new correlation values.
   - The report is the only GM-authored scheduler proof. Never write processed counts, `last*Ordinal` markers, catch-up baselines, or pending cycle counts into `progression_schedule.json`.

10. Final audit:
   - Remove stale Mortal World outputs.
   - Do not author `afterlife_notifications.json`; the client derives it from canonical receipts/state.

==================================================
COMBINED TURN DECISION TREE
==================================================

Use this decision tree when one turn contains several obligations at once: scheduler debt, catch-up, pending files, and a player action.

1. If `currentRealm = "Shining Abode"` and `preparedIncarnationPackage` is a valid bootstrap package object, stop ordinary afterlife processing and use pending-bootstrap handoff rules only. If the package is present but malformed or fails bootstrap validation, treat it as a package fault: preserve it and all pending Shining files for repair, and do not process ordinary Shining gameplay.
2. Otherwise, read `progressionControl` before selecting the scene. Record every due contour and every `afterlifeCatchupContours[]` value in `gm_thoughts_markdown`.
3. Read all pending afterlife control files before narration. A pending contract is not optional flavor; it must close through its exact canonical receipt/state surface.
4. Declare all changed Guardians, residents, Shining factions, gates, halls, and institutions in actor scope. Do not declare a non-actor environment as a relevant actor unless it has a matching reasoning block.
5. Resolve bounded catch-up first, then ordinary scheduler cycles, then pending contracts / direct player action. When a pending file is the client-authored mechanical form of the player action, close that contract as the action resolution and explain the ordering in reasoning.
6. Write only afterlife surfaces and exact receipts. Do not use Mortal World `worldEventsLog`, `factionDataChanges`, `UpdateNPCs`, `UpdateQuests`, `currentLocationData`, `timeChange`, `setWorldTime`, or `weatherChange`.
7. Finish with `progressionProcessingReport` when any afterlife cycle was due. Every processed contour uses its own count and own `newLast*Ordinal`; never copy the largest backlog marker to unrelated contours.

==================================================
1. VALID — CHAOS SEA WITH CATCH-UP, GUARDIAN PROJECT, RESIDENT REQUEST
==================================================

Scenario:
- The soul has returned to the Chaos Sea after a long mortal life.
- The player says: "Я иду в Обитель Азалии и спрашиваю Лиору, что она помнит о моей прошлой жизни."
- The scheduler says that Chaos Sea hub, Guardian projects, resident agency, and bounded catch-up are due.
- A resident history request is pending.

Input fragments:

```json
{
  "soul_state.json": {
    "currentRealm": "Chaos Sea",
    "enlightenment": 47,
    "inkFeathers": 118
  },
  "turn_request.json": {
    "sessionId": "afterlife_session_chaos_212",
    "requestId": "afterlife_turn_chaos_212",
    "turnNumber": 212,
    "playerAction": "Я иду в Обитель Азалии и спрашиваю Лиору, что она помнит о моей прошлой жизни.",
    "progressionControl": {
      "mustEvaluateChaosSeaProgression": true,
      "chaosSeaCyclesExpectedThisTurn": 1,
      "nextChaosSeaTurnOrdinal": 31,
      "mustEvaluateGuardianProjectProgression": true,
      "guardianProjectCyclesExpectedThisTurn": 2,
      "nextGuardianProjectCycleOrdinal": 18,
      "mustEvaluateResidentAgencyProgression": true,
      "residentAgencyCyclesExpectedThisTurn": 1,
      "nextResidentAgencyCycleOrdinal": 12,
      "afterlifeCatchupRequired": true,
      "afterlifeCatchupSummaryEventsRequired": 2,
      "afterlifeCatchupPressureTier": "major",
      "afterlifeCatchupContours": [
        "chaos_sea",
        "guardian_projects",
        "residents"
      ]
    }
  },
  "pending_guardian_abode_resident_interactions.json": {
    "requests": [
      {
        "requestId": "resident_history_212_liora",
        "residentId": "res_azalia_liora_001",
        "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
        "guardianName": "Азалия",
        "abodeId": "abode_azalia_memory_silk_001",
        "abodeName": "Обитель Шёлковой Памяти",
        "residentName": "Лиора",
        "interactionType": "history",
        "topic": "memory_of_previous_life",
        "createdAtTurn": 212,
        "createdAtUtc": "2026-04-24T08:35:00Z"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Correct GM reasoning:

```markdown
## Оценка времени и мира
- Realm: Chaos Sea.
- Lifecycle guards: no active ascension, no incarnation trigger, no Shining bootstrap package.
- progressionControl read before resolving the player action.
- Due contours: Chaos Sea hub x1, Guardian projects x2, resident agency x1.
- Catch-up: required, pressure tier major, summary outcomes required: 2.

## Охват NPC-анализа
- Режим: Mixed
- Релевантные акторы: Азалия, Лиора
- Почему они релевантны: Азалия владеет текущей Обителью и её проект продвигается; Лиора имеет pending history request.
- Контур среды: Море Хаоса имеет due hub/catch-up contour и описывается ниже в `Afterlife Living World`, но не объявляется actor без отдельного `###` reasoning block.
- Акторы вне охвата: остальные Хранители, прочие резиденты Обители Азалии
- Почему они вне охвата: их проекты и pending requests не затронуты этим ходом, и catch-up summary не требует их индивидуальных state mutations.

## Guardian Thoughts
### Азалия
- Ситуация: Душа вернулась после долгой смертной жизни и пришла к её резиденту.
- Внутренние мысли: Азалия видит, что Лиора готова раскрыть часть прошлого, но не хочет ломать память души слишком резко.
- Действия: Она позволяет разговор, усиливает защитный шелк Обители и продвигает проект мягкого раскрытия воспоминаний.

### Лиора
- Ситуация: Душа прямо спрашивает о её памяти прошлой жизни.
- Внутренние мысли: Лиора боится снова стать лишь тенью чужого прошлого, но хочет быть полезной.
- Действия: Она раскрывает один фрагмент истории и оставляет понятный след в резидентской памяти.

## Afterlife Living World / Живой мир Посмертия
- Catch-up outcome 1: за время отсутствия души в Море Хаоса усилились тихие течения воспоминаний; это отражается в hub-summary и не требует mortal worldEventsLog.
- Catch-up outcome 2: проект Азалии продвинулся, но не завершился; резиденты стали осторожнее относиться к резкому раскрытию прошлых жизней.
- Chaos Sea hub cycle processed: 1.
- Guardian project cycles processed: 2.
- Resident agency cycle processed: 1.
- Stable contours: Shining contours are not active because currentRealm is Chaos Sea and no Shining catch-up contour is listed.

## Отчёт scheduler
- progressionProcessingReport will close Chaos Sea ordinal 31, Guardian project final marker 18, Resident agency ordinal 12.
- afterlifeCatchupProcessed=true and summary events processed=2.
```

Correct response/state fragment:

```json
{
  "response": "Обитель Азалии встречает тебя шелестом тонких серебряных нитей. Лиора долго молчит, затем признаёт: в одной из твоих прошлых жизней она видела тебя не героем, а человеком, который однажды отказался от лёгкой победы ради чужой свободы. Азалия не вмешивается, но её шелк вокруг зала становится плотнее, чтобы память не разорвала душу слишком резко.",
  "gm_thoughts_markdown": "<see reasoning above>",
  "guardianProjectUpdates": [
    {
      "projectId": "gproj_azalia_memory_silk_001",
      "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
      "activeState": "active",

  "UpdateGuardianAbodeResidentHistoryLog": [

      "revealedAtTurn": 212,
      "revealedAtUtc": "2026-04-24T08:40:00Z"
    }
  ],
  "UpdateGuardianAbodeResidentInteractionReceipts": [
    {
      "requestId": "resident_history_212_liora",
      "residentId": "res_azalia_liora_001",
      "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
      "abodeId": "abode_azalia_memory_silk_001",
      "interactionType": "history",
      "historyEntryId": "hist_liora_212_previous_life",
      "status": "accepted",
      "resolvedAtTurn": 212,
      "resolvedAtUtc": "2026-04-24T08:40:00Z"
    }
  ],
  "progressionProcessingReport": {
    "sessionId": "afterlife_session_chaos_212",
    "requestId": "afterlife_turn_chaos_212",
    "turnNumber": 212,
    "chaosSeaCyclesProcessed": 1,
    "guardianProjectCyclesProcessed": 2,
    "residentAgencyCyclesProcessed": 1,
    "newLastChaosSeaSimulationOrdinal": 31,
    "newLastGuardianProjectCycleOrdinal": 18,
    "newLastResidentAgencyCycleOrdinal": 12,
    "afterlifeCatchupProcessed": true,
    "afterlifeCatchupSummaryEventsProcessed": 2
  }
}
```

Required CLI distribution:

```text
output/narrative_response.json
output/debug_logs.json
game_state/meta/guardian_projects.json
game_state/meta/guardian_abode_residents.json
game_state/control/progression_report.json
ready/turn_complete.json
```

Forbidden in this example:

```text
worldEventsLog
factionDataChanges
UpdateNPCs
NPCsInScene
UpdateQuests for mortal quests
currentLocationData
timeChange
weatherChange
UpdateInventory
afterlife_notifications.json manual writes
```

Why this is valid:
- The player action is a resident conversation, but scheduler debt is still processed first.
- Catch-up is summarized into exactly two outcomes.
- Resident history request is closed through an interaction receipt.
- The resident receives memory/history state, not NPC state.
- The Guardian project advances through project surfaces, not `UpdateGuardians.updateProject`.
- The report uses per-contour ordinals: Guardian project advances by 2, not by the max backlog of another contour.

==================================================
2. VALID — ACTIVE SHINING ABODE WITH CORE ACTION, FACTION, TRADE, RESIDENT AGENCY
==================================================

Scenario:
- The soul is in the active Shining Abode.
- The player says: "Откройте Врата Рассвета и пусть Орден Стеклянного Света подготовит торговую витрину."
- `shining_abode_state.json.availability = active` and there is no `preparedIncarnationPackage`, so this is ordinary active Shining Abode, not bootstrap handoff or sealed Shining state.
- Shining Abode, Shining faction, Shining trade, Guardian project, and resident agency cycles are due.
- A Shining core action request and a Shining trade inventory request are pending.

Input fragments:

```json
{
  "soul_state.json": {
    "currentRealm": "Shining Abode",
    "enlightenment": 100,
    "currentIncarnation": 9
  },
  "shining_abode_state.json": {
    "availability": "active",
    "preparedIncarnationPackage": null,
    "radiance": {
      "tier": 3,
      "pressure": "bright"
    },
    "gates": {
      "draftVersion": 4,
      "hasOpenDraft": false,
      "isStale": false,
      "allCandidateBlessingCards": [],
      "availableBlessingCards": [],
      "shownBlessingCardIds": [],
      "selectedBlessingCardIds": [],
      "nextCandidateCursor": 0,
      "rerollsRemaining": 0
    },
    "factions": [
      {
        "factionId": "shine_faction_glass_light_001",
        "originType": "native_radiant",
        "hallId": "hall_glass_light_001",
        "charter": {
          "factionName": "Орден Стеклянного Света",
          "favoredArchetype": "passage",
          "patronEffectFamily": "route",
          "summary": "Сияющая фракция, которая удерживает безопасные маршруты через рассветные врата и делает торговлю частью публичного порядка."
        },
        "leadership": {
          "leadershipState": "secure",
          "headActorType": "player_soul",
          "headActorId": "player_soul"
        },
        "baseStrength": 82,
        "factionStrength": 82,
        "investCountThisAscension": 0,
        "projectArchetypesCountedThisAscension": [],
        "projects": [],
        "tradeInventoryReceipts": [],
        "leadershipReceipts": [],
        "leadershipHistory": []
      }
    ],
    "coreActionReceipts": []
  },
  "turn_request.json": {
    "sessionId": "afterlife_session_shining_305",
    "requestId": "afterlife_turn_shining_305",
    "turnNumber": 305,
    "playerAction": "Откройте Врата Рассвета и пусть Орден Стеклянного Света подготовит торговую витрину.",
    "progressionControl": {
      "mustEvaluateShiningAbodeProgression": true,
      "shiningAbodeCyclesExpectedThisTurn": 1,
      "nextShiningAbodeCycleOrdinal": 22,
      "mustEvaluateShiningFactionProgression": true,
      "shiningFactionCyclesExpectedThisTurn": 2,
      "nextShiningFactionCycleOrdinal": 40,
      "mustEvaluateShiningTradeProgression": true,
      "shiningTradeCyclesExpectedThisTurn": 1,
      "nextShiningTradeCycleOrdinal": 9,
      "mustEvaluateGuardianProjectProgression": true,
      "guardianProjectCyclesExpectedThisTurn": 1,
      "nextGuardianProjectCycleOrdinal": 20,
      "mustEvaluateResidentAgencyProgression": true,
      "residentAgencyCyclesExpectedThisTurn": 1,
      "nextResidentAgencyCycleOrdinal": 13,
      "afterlifeCatchupRequired": false
    }
  },
  "pending_shining_abode_actions.json": {
    "requests": [
      {
        "requestId": "shine_core_open_gate_305",
        "actionType": "open_gates",
        "factionId": "shine_faction_glass_light_001",
        "factionName": "Орден Стеклянного Света",
        "radianceTierAtRequest": 3,
        "quotedCostFeathers": 0,
        "quotedCostLightSparks": 0,
        "sourceDraftVersion": 4,
        "selectedCardIds": [],
        "createdAtTurn": 305,
        "createdAtUtc": "2026-04-24T08:55:00Z"
      }
    ]
  },
  "pending_shining_trade_inventory_requests.json": {
    "requests": [
      {
        "requestId": "shine_trade_glass_305",
        "factionId": "shine_faction_glass_light_001",
        "factionName": "Орден Стеклянного Света",
        "tradeCycleId": "shining_return_9",
        "derivedTradeTier": 3,
        "derivedTradeSlotCount": 8,
        "derivedRarityCeiling": "radiant",
        "derivedServiceMultiplier": 1.5,
        "merchantProfile": "shining_faction",
        "createdAtTurn": 305,
        "createdAtUtc": "2026-04-24T08:55:00Z"
      }
    ]
  }

```

Correct GM reasoning:

```markdown
## Оценка времени и мира
- Realm: Shining Abode.
- `availability`: active, and `preparedIncarnationPackage`: null. This is ordinary active Shining Abode, not pending-bootstrap handoff or sealed Shining state.
- progressionControl read before narrative resolution.
- Due contours: Shining Abode x1, Shining faction x2, Shining trade x1, Guardian project x1, resident agency x1.
- Catch-up: not required.

## Охват NPC-анализа
- Режим: Mixed
- Релевантные акторы: Орден Стеклянного Света, Врата Рассвета, Мира, Элиан
- Почему они релевантны: Орден закрывает Shining trade request и получает faction progression; Врата Рассвета закрывают Shining core action; Мира меняет resident alignment; Элиан имеет due guardian project cycle.
- Акторы вне охвата: другие сияющие фракции, закрытые залы, прочие Хранители
- Почему они вне охвата: у них нет pending contracts, и два Shining faction cycles применены к текущей активной фракции, чьё действие запросил игрок.

## Reasoning / Размышления NPC / Guardian Thoughts
### Орден Стеклянного Света
- Ситуация: Душа просит открыть Врата и подготовить витрину через Орден.
- Внутренние мысли: Орден хочет показать пользу, но опасается слишком быстрого расширения влияния.
- Действия: Орден принимает поручение, усиливает дисциплину и открывает ограниченную торговую витрину.

### Врата Рассвета
- Ситуация: canonical gates draft был закрыт, но получил accepted `open_gates` core action request.
- Внутренние мысли: Врата удерживают сияющее давление под контролем и не раскрывают выбор благословений раньше подготовленного draft.
- Действия: `gates` обновляется как blessing-card draft container: новый `draftVersion`, `hasOpenDraft=true`, списки candidate/available cards и пустой `selectedBlessingCardIds[]`.

### Мира
- Ситуация: Резидентка видит рост Ордена и выбирает поддержать их публичный ритуал.
- Внутренние мысли: Мира считает, что поддержка Ордена даст ей более ясное место в сияющей иерархии.
- Действия: Её Shining alignment moves toward the Glass Light faction and is logged as resident agency.

### Элиан
- Ситуация: Проект Хранителя связан с устойчивостью сияющих переходов.
- Внутренние мысли: Элиан использует открытие Врат как редкий устойчивый момент для укрепления своего проекта.
- Действия: Проект получает один cycle progress because the gate action reinforces his domain.

## Afterlife Living World / Живой мир Посмертия
- Shining Abode cycle: public order brightens, the canonical gates draft opens with controlled pressure.
- Shining faction cycles: the Glass Light faction gains strength and lower restlessness through visible service.
- Shining trade cycle: the faction materializes explicit tradeInventory and closes the pending trade request.
- Guardian project cycle: one project advances.
- Resident agency cycle: Mira changes alignment/memory.

## Отчёт scheduler
- Shining Abode ordinal 22 processed.
- Shining faction final marker 40 processed.
- Shining trade ordinal 9 processed.
- Guardian project ordinal 20 processed.
- Resident agency ordinal 13 processed.
```

Why this is valid:

- The pending core action is closed through `coreActionReceipts[]`.
- The pending trade request is closed through faction `tradeInventory` plus faction `tradeInventoryReceipts[]`.
- If the player preview shows `expectedTradeInventory`, treat it as an audit scaffold for the canonical faction `tradeInventory`: keep `generationTradeTier`, `generationRarityCeiling`, `serviceMultiplierSnapshot`, slot count, `priceInFeathers`, and nested `relicData` aligned with the pending request.
- Every Shining trade `tradeInventory.items[].relicData.relicId` is unique in the inventory and absent from the player's existing `soulRelics.equipped[]` / `soulRelics.stored[]`; these ids become new Soul Relic identities on local purchase.
- Shining faction progress is not written through Mortal World faction fields.
- The resident is handled through resident afterlife state, not NPC state.
- The scheduler report advances each contour by its own expected count.

3. VALID — SHINING ABODE PENDING-BOOTSTRAP HANDOFF
==================================================

Scenario:
- `currentRealm = "Shining Abode"`.
- `shining_abode_state.json.preparedIncarnationPackage` is not null.
- The player has already chosen a prepared next-life package.
- No unresolved afterlife pending/control contracts remain; Guardian, resident, archive, offering, Shining core/trade/politics, malformed wrong-realm NPC, and legacy `pendingNativeFactionDiscovery` blockers must be resolved or repaired before this handoff, not in the same response.
- The current accepted turn must create `TriggerIncarnation` while preserving the frozen package for the client transition code to consume after successful mortal bootstrap.
- `TriggerIncarnation` is valid only because this is the current accepted turn with matching validated `game_state/control/pending_turn_snapshot` plus client-owned `game_state/control/pending_turn_snapshot.authority.json` authority; it is not a manual/out-of-band file the GM can create later.
- Ordinary Shining living-world progression must NOT run in this handoff turn.

Correct GM decision:

```markdown
## Оценка времени и мира
- Realm: Shining Abode.
- preparedIncarnationPackage is present.
- Mode: Shining Abode pending-bootstrap handoff.
- No unresolved afterlife pending/control contracts are present; if any such blocker exists, Soul Gates must stop here and the GM must not combine its closure with bootstrap.
- Ordinary Shining living-world progression is suppressed; the expected ordinary Shining cycle counts are zero in this handoff request.
- This turn only emits `TriggerIncarnation` from the frozen next-life package. The GM must preserve the package; the client transition code consumes and clears it only after successful mortal bootstrap.
- Accepted-turn authority: `TriggerIncarnation` is authored only for this accepted turn and relies on the matching validated `game_state/control/pending_turn_snapshot` plus client-owned `game_state/control/pending_turn_snapshot.authority.json`; if that authority is missing or stale, the runtime treats the trigger as invalid (`incarnation_trigger_invalid_validated_snapshot_context`).

## Охват NPC-анализа
- Режим: Scene-local
- Релевантные акторы: нет
- Почему они релевантны: Ход является lifecycle/bootstrap handoff and does not mutate Guardian/resident/faction agency.
- Акторы вне охвата: сияющие фракции, Хранители, резиденты
- Почему они вне охвата: pending-bootstrap handoff explicitly suppresses ordinary Shining progression.

## Reasoning / Размышления NPC / Guardian Thoughts
- No actor reasoning block is required because no structured actor updates are emitted.
```

Correct response/control fragment:

```json
{
  "response": "Сияющая Обитель не требует новых решений. Она только удерживает уже выбранный узор, пока душа проходит сквозь подготовленные врата. Стеклянный рассвет сжимается в точку памяти, и впереди раскрывается смертный мир храмовых архивов и речных духов.",

  "TriggerIncarnation": {
    "worldDescription": "Мир поздней бронзы, где города-государства спорят с духами рек.",
    "characterDescription": "Ребёнок писца, родившийся с памятью о стеклянном рассвете.",
    "circumstances": "Семья служит храмовому архиву на границе войны и дипломатии.",
    "source": "shining_prepared_package"
  }

```

Correct Shining state handling:

Do not write a `shining_abode_state.json` mutation for `preparedIncarnationPackage` in this handoff turn. Preserve the existing package object exactly as it was in input; the runtime reads it after accepting `TriggerIncarnation`, materializes the frozen blessing/world setup, and clears it after successful mortal bootstrap. Do not add unsupported handoff marker fields.

There is no required Shining state output fragment for this handoff unless another already-valid state field must be preserved unchanged by the normal save pipeline.

Do not create `game_state/control/incarnation_trigger.json` outside the accepted-turn lifecycle. The runtime requires the matching validated `game_state/control/pending_turn_snapshot` context; a trigger without that authority is ignored/deleted rather than consumed.

Do NOT write in this handoff example:

```text
preparedIncarnationPackage: null
progressionProcessingReport for ordinary Shining cycles
shining_abode_state faction/radiance/trade progression unrelated to the package
guardianProjectUpdates
residentThoughtJournalUpdates
factionDataChanges
worldEventsLog
currentLocationData
timeChange
weatherChange
UpdateNPCs
```

Why this is valid:
- `preparedIncarnationPackage != null` changes the mode.
- The scheduler's ordinary Shining debt is not processed because the turn is a lifecycle handoff, not an ordinary active Shining Abode turn.
- The GM does not invent new Shining events while bootstrapping the next mortal life.

4. VALID — CHAOS SEA GUARDIAN TRADE REQUEST
==================================================

Scenario:
- Realm is `Chaos Sea`.
- The player tries to open the current Guardian's trade panel, but the client found missing/stale authored stock.
- `game_state/control/pending_guardian_trade_request.json` is present.
- There is no scheduler debt in this small example. If scheduler debt is also due, process it first and add `progressionProcessingReport` as in example 1.

Input fragment:

```json
{
  "turn_request.json": {
    "sessionId": "afterlife_session_trade_240",
    "requestId": "afterlife_turn_trade_240",
    "turnNumber": 240,
    "playerAction": "Я прошу Азалию открыть витрину Обители.",
    "progressionControl": {
      "mustEvaluateChaosSeaProgression": false,
      "mustEvaluateGuardianProjectProgression": false,
      "mustEvaluateResidentAgencyProgression": false,
      "afterlifeCatchupRequired": false
    }
  },
  "pending_guardian_trade_request.json": {
    "requestId": "guardian_trade_azalia_return_5",
    "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
    "guardianName": "Азалия",
    "abodeId": "abode_azalia_memory_silk_001",
    "returnCycleId": "return_5",
    "currentReputation": 65,
    "derivedTradeSlotCount": 2,
    "effectiveRarityCeilingBonusSteps": 0,
    "projectBonusSignature": "0|0|0",
    "createdAtUtc": "2026-04-24T09:00:00Z",
    "createdAtTurn": 240
  }
}
```

Correct GM procedure:
- Read the pending request exactly; do not change `requestId`, `guardianId`, `abodeId`, `returnCycleId`, slot count, or project bonus signature.
- Materialize `guardian.tradeInventory` for the same Guardian in `game_state/meta/guardians.json`; do not put trade stock into a commandless `UpdateGuardians` entry.
- `tradeInventory.tradeCycleId` must equal `returnCycleId`.
- `generationReputationTier` and `pricingReputationTier` must be derived from `currentReputation`; here `65` means `Friendly`.
- `items.length` must equal `derivedTradeSlotCount`.
- Every `tradeInventory.items[].slotId` must be unique inside this inventory. The client buys by `slotId`; duplicates make the purchase target ambiguous and are invalid.
- Close through `UpdateGuardianTradeInventoryReceipts`; matching stock without a ready receipt is invalid.
- Do not write `afterlife_notifications.json`; the client derives the ready inbox event.
- The fragment below shows the canonical persisted Guardian state patch; preserve all other fields on the existing Guardian object.

Correct response/state fragment:

```json
{
  "response": "Азалия проводит ладонью по шёлковым полкам, и две реликвии проявляются в мягком серебряном свете. Витрина Обители готова; теперь душа может осмотреть товары через интерфейс торговли Хранителя.",
```

Forbidden in this example:
- `UpdateInventory` for Soul Relics.
- `afterlife_notifications.json` manual writes.
- `pending_guardian_trade_request.json` mutation in GM output.
- `tradeInventory.items.length` different from `derivedTradeSlotCount`.
- duplicated `tradeInventory.items[].slotId`.
- ready receipt with a different `requestId`, `abodeId`, or `tradeCycleId`.

==================================================
5. VALID — CHAOS SEA ARCHIVE CONSULTATION AND PROJECT FUEL REQUESTS
==================================================

Scenario:
- Realm is `Chaos Sea`.
- The player uses reserved afterlife archive entries.
- Archive action pending files are request contracts; they close through `archiveActionResolutions` in `soul_state.json`.
- This section shows two compatible accepted resolutions. The consultation is resolved by Azalia through a same-turn completed `lore_research` project; the project-fuel request targets a different Guardian's already-active project to avoid the one-active-project-per-Guardian limit.

Input fragments:

```json
{
  "pending_archive_consultation_request.json": {
    "requestId": "archive_consult_azalia_241",
    "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
    "guardianName": "Азалия",
    "archiveId": "archive_life_7_broken_oath",
    "archiveTitle": "Клятва, нарушенная у северных ворот",

    "archiveRarity": "Rare",
    "archiveSourceKind": "codex",
    "targetIncarnation": 8,
    "createdAtTurn": 241,
    "createdAtUtc": "2026-04-24T09:20:00Z",
    "requestedMode": "consultation"
  },
  "pending_archive_project_fuel_request.json": {
    "requestId": "archive_fuel_velian_242",
    "guardianId": "guard_craft_velian_001",
    "guardianName": "Велиан",
    "archiveId": "archive_life_5_mirror_forge",
    "archiveTitle": "Зеркальная кузница шестой жизни",
    "archiveEntryType": "secret_record",
    "archiveRarity": "Epic",
    "archiveSourceKind": "codex",
    "targetProjectId": "gproj_velian_mirror_forge_001",
    "targetProjectName": "Зеркальная кузница откликов",
    "createdAtTurn": 242,
    "createdAtUtc": "2026-04-24T09:22:00Z",
    "requestedMode": "project_fuel"
  }
}
```

Correct GM procedure:
- For consultation, return `archiveActionResolutions` with exact `requestId`, `archiveId`, `requestedMode = consultation`, `status`, `guardianId`, and at least one machine-readable outcome field.
- Accepted consultation must also create a completed `lore_research` Guardian project with `projectOrigin = archive_consultation`, `consultationRequestId`, `consultationArchiveId`, and `projectOutcomeAudit` matching the resolution fields.
- If that consultation creates a Guardian quest hook, the quest record in `questManagement.availableQuests`/`activeQuests`/`completedQuests` must carry `questOrigin = archive_consultation_hook` and `sourceProjectId = archive_consult_project_azalia_241`. Ordinary lore-research hooks use `questOrigin = lore_research_hook`; special lore-research lines use `questOrigin = lore_research_special_line`.
- For ordinary Wary/Neutral-or-better Guardian scenes that are not backed by lore/archive tokens, the GM may offer a voluntary / добровольное future mortal-life assignment with `questOrigin = guardian_baseline_mortal_life_hook`. Keep it in `availableQuests` until the player explicitly accepts it; do not create an `activeQuest` just because the offer exists, and do not treat an empty `availableQuests` array as a validation error.
- For project fuel, return `archiveActionResolutions` with exact `requestId`, `archiveId`, `requestedMode = project_fuel`, `targetProjectId`, `status`, `resultMode`, and `resultAmount > 0`.
- For project fuel, `archiveEntryType = lore_fragment` may resolve only as `resultMode = project_work`; `archiveEntryType = secret_record` may resolve only as `resultMode = pressure_relief`.
- Accepted archive entries are consumed/resolved by canonical archive state; rejected/cancelled entries return to availability.
- If project fuel is accepted, also reflect the exact effect in Guardian project state/journal. `pressure_relief` reduces pressure or improves stability; it does not advance `workDone`/`currentStage`.
- Do not invent mortal quests, mortal inventory, or manual inbox notifications.

Correct response surface:


Guardian project sabotage is also a structured project update. If the GM records a hostile sabotage result, carry `sabotageAudit` on the exact `guardianProjectUpdates[]` entry and reflect the Abode Power side effect as a `guardianPowerEvents.reasonType = rival_strike` event. The negative `delta` is derived from `sabotageSeverityScore`: `3..4 = -1`, `5..7 = -2`, `8+ = -4`.

Exceptional/system archive update shape:

Use `afterlifeArchiveUpdates` only when a system or exceptional afterlife reward creates/removes archive entries directly. Do not use it to close `pending_archive_consultation_request.json` or `pending_archive_project_fuel_request.json`; those close through `archiveActionResolutions`.

```json
{
  "afterlifeArchiveUpdates": [
    {
      "command": "add",
      "entry": {
        "archiveId": "archive_system_azalia_silk_warning_001",
        "entryType": "lore_fragment",
        "title": "Предупреждение шёлковой памяти",
        "summary": "Азалия оставила системный архивный фрагмент о том, как безопасно читать опасные воспоминания.",
        "rarity": "rare",
        "sourceLife": 8,
        "sourceKind": "system",
        "sourceGuardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
        "sourceGuardianName": "Азалия",
        "sourceEntryId": "guardian_social_entry_azalia_lore_316",
        "tags": [
          "afterlife",
          "guardian_lore"
        ],

      }

      "command": "remove",
      "archiveId": "archive_obsolete_duplicate_001"
    }
  ]

```

Notification rule:
- Do not write `afterlife_notifications.json`.
- The client derives notifications from canonical archive action receipts, Guardian trade ready receipts, resident roster/interaction/transfer receipts, Guardian social entries, Shining core/trade receipts, and later consumed `pendingShiningBlessingEffects`.

==================================================
6. VALID — ORDINARY CHAOS SEA TRIGGERINCARNATION
==================================================

Scenario:
- Realm is ordinary `Chaos Sea`.
- No Shining `preparedIncarnationPackage` is present.
- The player explicitly chooses the next mortal life.
- This is not Life Evaluation and not Shining pending-bootstrap handoff.
- This response is part of the accepted turn that owns the matching validated `game_state/control/pending_turn_snapshot`; a later manual `incarnation_trigger.json` repair without that authority is invalid.

Correct response/control fragment:

```json
{
  "response": "Море Хаоса раскрывает новую смертную нить. Хранители не спорят с выбором души: впереди мир заснеженных перевалов, караванных обетов и древних горных архивов.",
  "TriggerIncarnation": {
    "worldDescription": "Горный мир позднего средневековья, где караванные города держатся на клятвах, архивных печатях и договорах с духами перевалов.",
    "characterDescription": "Ребёнок хранителя перевального архива, родившийся в семье, которая помнит долги караванов.",
    "circumstances": "Первая зима жизни начинается во время спора между городскими старейшинами и духом закрытого перевала."
  }
}
```

Do not write in ordinary Chaos Sea `TriggerIncarnation`:
- `TriggerLifeEnd`.
- Life Evaluation rewards.
- `preparedIncarnationPackage` mutations.
- Shining faction/radiance/trade progression.
- Mortal World `currentLocationData`, `worldEventsLog`, `UpdateNPCs`, `UpdateQuests`, or weather/time outputs.
- Out-of-band `incarnation_trigger.json` after the turn has ended or after snapshot/terminal authority is gone.

Guardian-forced incarnation is a different flow. It is legal only on an ordinary player-driven Chaos Sea turn with either legacy explicit provocation against the current active Guardian or current-turn resolved afterlife spiritual conflict proof. Guardian identity, current Abode, reputation threshold, and severity are evaluated from the validated pre-turn `game_state/meta/guardians.json` snapshot; same-turn Guardian edits cannot make the force legal. It must include the extra guardian-forced fields described in `TaskGuides/CLI_Step_Main.txt`.

6A. VALID — LEGACY GUARDIAN PROVOCATION TAG EVIDENCE
---------------------------------------------

The runtime accepts exact `playerAction` tags as deterministic evidence for Guardian-forced incarnation. This is the input context, not a GM response surface:

Additional pre-state requirement:
- The provoking Guardian must be the current active Guardian.
- Pre-turn `game_state/meta/guardians.json` for that Guardian must have `relationshipData.currentReputation` / `CurrentReputation <= -21`.
- Severity mapping is mechanical: `-21..-50 => "harsh"`, `-51..-100 => "severe"`. A reputation of `-20` or better must not force incarnation even with a provocation tag.

```text
input/turn_request.json:
playerAction = "[GUARDIAN_PROVOCATION: guardian_azalia] Игрок deliberately insults Азалия and demands punishment."
turnNumber = 77

pre-turn activeGuardian.guardianId = "guardian_azalia"
pre-turn guardian_azalia.relationshipData.currentReputation = -34
```

Correct GM response:

```json
{
  "TriggerIncarnation": {
    "source": "guardian_forced",
    "guardianId": "guardian_azalia",
    "severityBand": "harsh",
    "reason": "The player explicitly provoked the current Guardian.",
    "provocationSummary": "The player used the GUARDIAN_PROVOCATION tag and insulted Azalia directly.",
    "worldDescription": "A cold frontier world where oaths have immediate metaphysical weight.",
    "characterDescription": "A child born under a debt-mark, watched by oath-keepers.",
    "circumstances": "The first winter begins with a public obligation that cannot be ignored."
  }
}
```

Why this is valid:
- `[GUARDIAN_PROVOCATION]` without an id is accepted as explicit provocation evidence.
- `[GUARDIAN_PROVOCATION: guardianId]` is accepted only when the id exactly matches `TriggerIncarnation.guardianId`.
- `severityBand = "harsh"` matches pre-turn reputation `-34` in the `-21..-50` band. If the same Guardian had `currentReputation = -62`, the correct `severityBand` would be `"severe"`.
- This tag is evidence, not a pending file. It does not authorize Shining handoff, protected post-life turns, or forcing one Guardian's punishment through another Guardian's id.
- The trigger is still valid only inside the current accepted turn with matching validated `game_state/control/pending_turn_snapshot` authority; otherwise validation reports `incarnation_trigger_invalid_validated_snapshot_context`.

6B. VALID — ASCENSION HANDOFF IS CLIENT-EXECUTED
------------------------------------------------

Ascension is a Chaos Sea lifecycle trigger. The GM confirms the explicit choice and writes the trigger only; the client performs the later realm handoff after validation. Do not manually switch `soul_state.currentRealm` to `Shining Abode` in the same response.

Input prerequisites:
- `soul_state.currentRealm = "Chaos Sea"`.
- Enlightenment is ascension-ready: `enlightenment.experience >= 60` or `soulProgression.totalExperience >= 60`; legacy max/Transcendence markers (`progressPercent=100`, high tier, `Transcendence`) remain valid.
- `playerAction` explicitly chooses Ascension.
- No `TriggerLifeEnd`, no Shining pending-bootstrap package handoff, no Mortal World state mutation.

Correct accepted response:

```json
{
  "AscensionTrigger": true,
  "playerChoice": "Ascension",
  "response": "Море Хаоса раскрывается сияющим разломом. Душа делает выбор вознестись; дальнейший переход в Сияющую Обитель выполнит клиент после принятия этого хода."
}
```

Correct `game_state/control/ascension.json` after distribution:

```json
{
  "AscensionTrigger": true,
  "playerChoice": "Ascension"
}
```

Forbidden in the same accepted response:
- `TriggerLifeEnd`.
- `game_state/control/life_transitions.json`.
- `TriggerIncarnation`.
- Manual `soul_state.currentRealm = "Shining Abode"`.
- Shining faction/radiance/trade state mutations.
- `progressionProcessingReport` unless the same request's `progressionControl` explicitly requires scheduler debt.
- Mortal World `currentLocationData`, `UpdateNPCs`, `worldEventsLog`, or faction state.
- Mortal bootstrap files or current-world lore. The client performs the Shining Abode handoff after accepting `ascension.json`.

==================================================
7. VALID — DIRECT CHAOS SEA GACHA VS GUARDIAN-MEDIATED GACHA
==================================================

Direct Chaos Sea `/gacha`:
- Player action contains `[CHAOS_SEA_DIRECT_GACHA]` and an exact cost phrase such as `тратит 25 Чернильных Перьев`.
- Does not use `UpdateGuardians.processGacha`.
- Does not consume Guardian per-return charges.
- The client already spent the Ink Feathers before the GM turn; preserve the post-spend balance and do not write `metaStateUpdates.inkFeatherChanges.spend`.
- `turn_request.json.playerAction` must contain the exact prepaid cost phrase `<N> Чернильных Перьев` or `<N> Ink Feathers`; the validator extracts direct-gacha cost from that phrase.
- Must materialize exactly one new Soul Relic in Soul/meta state.
- Must read the full `turn_request.json.gachaBaseResult` audit (`diceUsed`, `baseScore`, `baseRarity`, `formula`) before choosing the relic.
- Final rarity must equal `turn_request.json.gachaBaseResult.baseRarity` exactly. Direct `/gacha` has no upgrade or downgrade path.
- No other `soul_state.json` changes are allowed: existing Soul Relics keep their exact JSON, unrelated soul fields keep their pre-turn values, and the only allowed diff is the already-paid feather balance plus one appended relic.

Correct direct `/gacha` response fragment:

```json
{
  "metaStateUpdates": {
    "soulRelicOperations": {
      "addRelic": {
        "relicId": "soul_relic_direct_chaos_glass_eye_001",
        "name": "Стеклянный Глаз Течения",
        "rarity": "Rare",
        "description": "Реликвия Моря Хаоса, показывающая один скрытый поворот судьбы до начала следующей жизни."
      }
    }
  },
  "response": "Море Хаоса принимает уже списанную плату. Из пены выходит стеклянный глаз, внутри которого медленно вращается будущий перевал."
}
```

7A. REFERENCE — CHAOS SEA ABODE TRAVEL
--------------------------------------

`[CHAOS_SEA_TRAVEL]` is afterlife navigation, not Mortal World movement. The player action should already provide the target abode/guardian and previous navigation context. The target abode must already be present in the validated pre-turn `game_state/meta/guardians.json.chaosSeaNavigation.discoveredAbodes[]`, and the target Guardian's pre-turn `abode.isDiscovered` must already be `true`. GM must keep the canonical navigation roots synchronized in the accepted `game_state/meta/guardians.json`. Do not redirect the soul to a different discovered abode, and do not make a stale/manual travel legal by adding the target to `discoveredAbodes` during the same turn: accepted state must use the exact `targetAbodeId` and `targetGuardianId` from `playerAction`.

Canonical `game_state/meta/guardians.json patch` view. This is not an `UpdateGuardians` command: preserve the full existing Guardian objects in the actual file and change only the shown canonical roots/fields.

```json
{
  "guardians": [
    {
      "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
      "...": "preserve every existing canonical Guardian field",
      "abode": {
        "abodeId": "abode_azalia_silk_hall",
        "...": "preserve every existing canonical Abode field",
        "name": "Шёлковый Зал Азалии",
        "isDiscovered": true
      }
    }
  ],
  "activeGuardian": {
    "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001"
  },
  "chaosSeaNavigation": {
    "currentAbodeId": "abode_azalia_silk_hall",
    "discoveredAbodes": [
      "abode_old_lantern",
      "abode_azalia_silk_hall"
    ]
  }
}
```

Narrative `response` should describe the afterlife arrival and the target Guardian's reaction, for example: "Море Хаоса разворачивает путь к Шёлковому Залу Азалии. Обитель узнаёт душу по шелесту нитей, а Азалия встречает её как нового текущего Хранителя этого места."

Do not write for `[CHAOS_SEA_TRAVEL]`:
- Mortal World `currentLocationData`.
- `UpdateNPCs`, `NPCsInScene`, or `worldEventsLog`.
- Travel time/weather changes.
- Navigation that changes `chaosSeaNavigation.currentAbodeId` without matching `activeGuardian`.
- Same-turn discovery of the target abode. If the target was not in pre-turn `discoveredAbodes`, refuse/repair the travel instead of adding it and moving there.

Guardian-mediated gacha:
- Uses `UpdateGuardians.processGacha`.
- Requires remaining Guardian gacha charges for the current return.
- Must also write matching `metaStateUpdates.inkFeatherChanges.spend` and `metaStateUpdates.soulRelicOperations.addRelic`.
- Rarity may only rise through Abode Power rarity ceiling bonus and completed `relic_forging` project bonus.
- Guardian reputation affects charges/trade pricing only; it does not add rarity upgrade chance.
- Hard/Impossible mortal-world difficulty does not add afterlife Guardian gacha rarity steps.
- If rarity is upgraded above the client base, include `gachaBonusAudit`. It is safe to include the audit with zero bonus steps even when rarity is unchanged.
- The accepted Guardian state must increment `gachaSystem.chargesUsedThisReturn` and append a `gachaHistory[]` entry.

Correct guardian-mediated response fragment:

```json
{
  "UpdateGuardians": [
    {
      "command": "processGacha",
      "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
      "inkFeathersSpent": 120,
      "result": {
        "relicId": "soul_relic_azalia_lantern_thread_001",
        "name": "Фонарь Шёлковой Нити",
        "rarity": "Uncommon",
        "description": "Реликвия Азалии, позволяющая мягче вспомнить одну связь между жизнями."
      },
      "gachaBonusAudit": {
        "baseRarity": "Uncommon",
        "finalRarity": "Uncommon",
        "abodePowerBonusSteps": 0,
        "relicForgingBonusSteps": 0
      }
    }
  ],
  "metaStateUpdates": {
    "inkFeatherChanges": {
      "spend": 120
    },
    "soulRelicOperations": {
      "addRelic": {
        "relicId": "soul_relic_azalia_lantern_thread_001",
        "name": "Фонарь Шёлковой Нити",
        "rarity": "Uncommon",
        "description": "Реликвия Азалии, позволяющая мягче вспомнить одну связь между жизнями."
      }
    }
  },
  "response": "Азалия берёт Перья и вплетает их в тонкую нить. На конце нити загорается маленький фонарь, не яркий, но устойчивый."
}
```

Canonical Guardian state impact, shown as a patch fragment:

```json
{
  "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
  "gachaSystem": {
    "chargesPerReturn": 2,
    "chargesUsedThisReturn": 1,
    "gachaHistory": [
      {
        "timestamp": "2026-04-24T09:40:00Z",
        "costInFeathers": 120,
        "relicId": "soul_relic_azalia_lantern_thread_001",
        "finalRarity": "Uncommon"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

==================================================
8. VALID — AFTERLIFE ABODE OFFERING
==================================================

Scenario:
- Realm is `Chaos Sea` or `Shining Abode`.
- Player action contains `[INK_FEATHER_ACTION: ABODE_OFFERING]`.
- `game_state/control/pending_abode_offering.json` exists and targets a specific Guardian and return cycle.
- This example is only for `offeringType = ink_feathers`; non-feather offerings use plain `[ABODE_OFFERING]`.
- For `offeringType = soul_relic`, the client snapshots a canonical `relicRarity` from the stored Soul Relic fields in this order: `rarity`, then `quality`, then `relicRarity`. The GM must preserve that pending `relicRarity` in `guardianPowerEvents[].audit.relicRarity` and must not silently downgrade a `quality`-only or `relicRarity`-only relic to `common`.
- The GM must also read `Examples/E_CLI_Ink_Feather_Actions.txt`, section `VALID — ABODE_OFFERING`.

Input fragment:

```json
{
  "turn_request.json": {
    "sessionId": "afterlife_session_offering_250",
    "requestId": "afterlife_turn_offering_250",
    "turnNumber": 250,
    "playerAction": "[INK_FEATHER_ACTION: ABODE_OFFERING] Я отдаю 100 Чернильных Перьев Обители Азалии."
  },
  "pending_abode_offering.json": {
    "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
    "guardianName": "Азалия",
    "offeringType": "ink_feathers",
    "inkFeathersOffered": 100,
    "returnCycleId": "return_5",
    "createdAtUtc": "2026-04-24T09:50:00Z"
  }
}
```

Correct response/output fragment:

```json
{
  "guardianPowerEvents": [
    {
      "eventId": "abode_offering_azalia_return_5",
      "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
      "delta": 2,
      "reasonType": "offering",
      "sourceSurface": "guardianAbodeOffering",
      "sourceId": "return_5",
      "title": "Подношение усилило Обитель",
      "summary": "Душа отдала 100 Чернильных Перьев Обители Азалии.",
      "audit": {
        "offeringType": "ink_feathers",
        "inkFeathersOffered": 100,
        "returnCycleId": "return_5",
        "capRemainingBefore": 150,
        "baseDelta": 2,
        "finalDelta": 2
      }
    }
  ],
  "response": "Перья растворяются в шёлковых стенах Обители. На мгновение вся память Азалии становится плотнее, и её залы получают новый слой защиты."
}
```

Required `output/ink_feather_action_result.json` because this pending offering has `offeringType = ink_feathers`.
The receipt keeps `actionTag = ABODE_OFFERING`; the matching `guardianPowerEvents[].sourceSurface` remains the canonical `guardianAbodeOffering`.

```json
{
  "sessionId": "afterlife_session_offering_250",
  "requestId": "afterlife_turn_offering_250",
  "turnNumber": 250,
  "actionTag": "ABODE_OFFERING",
  "resolved": true,
  "costInFeathers": 100,
  "resolutionType": "abodeOffering",
  "summary": "The Abode absorbs the offering and gains power.",
  "stateEvidence": {
    "affectedFiles": [
      "game_state/meta/guardians.json",
      "game_state/meta/abode_power_journal.json"
    ],
    "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
    "powerGain": 2,
    "returnCycleId": "return_5",
    "powerEventId": "abode_offering_azalia_return_5",
    "reasonType": "offering"
  }
}
```

Non-feather `soul_relic` offering uses the same Abode Power event surface but no Ink Feather receipt. The pending request is created by the client after reserving the stored relic; the GM must echo the pending `relicId`, `relicName`, and canonical `relicRarity` exactly.

```json
{
  "guardianPowerEvents": [
    {
      "eventId": "abode_offering_azalia_soul_relic_return_5",
      "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
      "delta": 2,
      "reasonType": "offering",
      "sourceSurface": "guardianAbodeOffering",
      "sourceId": "return_5",
      "title": "Реликвия стала опорой Обители",
      "summary": "Душа принесла Азалии редкую реликвию, и её шёлковые залы получили новый устойчивый узел памяти.",
      "audit": {
        "offeringType": "soul_relic",
        "relicId": "soul_relic_lantern_memory_rare_001",
        "relicName": "Фонарь бережной памяти",
        "relicRarity": "Rare",
        "returnCycleId": "return_5",
        "baseDelta": 2,
        "finalDelta": 2
      }
    }
  ],
  "response": "Азалия принимает реликвию не как плату, а как якорь. Фонарь остаётся в её Обители, и каждая нить вокруг зала начинает помнить дорогу домой."
}
```

Non-feather archive offering works the same way. The GM closes it through `guardianPowerEvents` only, and the archive-specific audit must preserve `archiveId`, `archiveTitle`, `archiveEntryType`, `archiveRarity`, and `returnCycleId` from `pending_abode_offering.json`.

```json
{
  "guardianPowerEvents": [
    {
      "eventId": "abode_offering_azalia_archive_return_5",
      "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
      "delta": 2,
      "reasonType": "offering",
      "sourceSurface": "guardianAbodeOffering",
      "sourceId": "return_5",
      "title": "Архивный фрагмент укрепил Обитель",
      "summary": "Душа отдала Азалии редкий фрагмент знания; Обитель получила новый слой защищённой памяти.",
      "audit": {
        "offeringType": "archive_lore_fragment",
        "archiveId": "archive_life_7_broken_oath",
        "archiveTitle": "Клятва, нарушенная у северных ворот",
        "archiveEntryType": "lore_fragment",
        "archiveRarity": "Rare",
        "returnCycleId": "return_5",
        "baseDelta": 2,
        "finalDelta": 2
      }
    }
  ],
  "response": "Фрагмент знания растворяется в стенах зала как тонкая запись. Азалия не превращает его в смертный квест; он остаётся силой Обители."
}
```

Forbidden:
- Treating `ABODE_OFFERING` as `GUARDIAN_FAVOR`.
- Ignoring `pending_abode_offering.json`.
- Using another Guardian or another return cycle.
- Writing only narrative without `guardianPowerEvents.reasonType = offering`.
- Writing `output/ink_feather_action_result.json` for non-feather offerings such as `soul_relic`, `archive_lore_fragment`, or `archive_secret_record`.
- For `soul_relic`, recomputing rarity from another field, defaulting missing rarity to `common`, or writing an audit `relicRarity` different from `pending_abode_offering.json.relicRarity`.

==================================================
9. VALID — SHINING FACTION FOUNDING, REALIGNMENT, LEADERSHIP
==================================================

This section contains three independent Shining political pending-contract examples. Each example is a patch fragment; preserve all other required roots of `shining_abode_state.json` and `guardian_abode_residents.json`.

Political request identity and locks:
- Every active `requests[]` entry in `pending_shining_faction_foundings.json`, `pending_shining_faction_realignments.json`, `pending_shining_faction_leadership_transitions.json`, and `pending_shining_trade_inventory_requests.json` must have a unique non-empty `requestId` inside that file. Resolved notification identity is keyed by request family plus `requestId`; duplicate ids can hide a different contract and are invalid.
- `pending_shining_faction_foundings.json` may contain only one unresolved player-soul founding request. Every accepted founding makes `player_soul` the head of the new faction, so two simultaneous founding requests conflict with the single-head invariant even when their proposed faction/hall ids differ.
- Leadership `candidateHeadActorType/headActorId` and `incumbentHeadActorType/headActorId` are locked actor identities, not decorative labels. A resident candidate or incumbent must not simultaneously be in pending Shining realignment, ordinary inter-Abode resident transfer, founding supporter set, or another leadership transition. If a conflict exists, preserve the pending files for repair; do not close the leadership request by inventing a winner.
- Non-resident leadership actors (`player_soul`, `guardian`, `radiant_actor`) still must not be referenced by another pending leadership transition unless it is the same faction/request being resolved.

9A. Founding a new Shining faction:

Resource reservation rule:
- Creating `pending_shining_faction_foundings.json` is a client-local reservation: the client has already deducted `quotedCostFeathers = 25` from `soul_state.inkFeathers.current` and `quotedCostLightSparks = 15` from `shining_abode_state.json.lightSparks` before the GM receives the turn.
- The live request must include `reservedInkFeathersBefore` and `reservedLightSparksBefore`. The current state must equal `reservedInkFeathersBefore - quotedCostFeathers` and `reservedLightSparksBefore - quotedCostLightSparks`; if those numbers do not line up, preserve the pending file for repair instead of founding a faction.
- On `status = accepted`, do not restore pre-reservation resource balances. The accepted response may spend additional resources only if another valid same-turn Shining contract explicitly authorizes that spend, but it must never make current Feathers/Sparks higher than the validated pre-turn reserved balances.

Input fragment:

```json
{
  "pending_shining_faction_foundings.json": {
    "requests": [
      {
        "requestId": "shining_found_dawn_chorus_301",
        "proposedFactionId": "faction_dawn_chorus",
        "proposedHallId": "hall_dawn_chorus",
        "proposedHallName": "Зал Рассветного Хора",
        "proposedHallDescription": "Сияющий зал, где резиденты договариваются о мягком сопровождении будущих жизней.",
        "proposedHallServiceTags": [
          "social",
          "memory"
        ],
        "charter": {
          "factionName": "Рассветный Хор",
          "favoredArchetype": "accord",
          "patronEffectFamily": "social",
          "summary": "Фракция поддерживает согласие между резидентами, Хранителями и будущими воплощениями."
        },
        "supportingResidentIds": [
          "res_mira_001",
          "res_liora_001",
          "res_tavian_001"
        ],
        "quotedCostFeathers": 25,
        "quotedCostLightSparks": 15,
        "reservedInkFeathersBefore": 120,
        "reservedLightSparksBefore": 40,
        "createdAtTurn": 301,
        "createdAtUtc": "2026-04-24T10:10:00Z"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Correct Shining state patch:

```json
{
  "halls": [
    {
      "hallId": "hall_dawn_chorus",
      "hallName": "Зал Рассветного Хора",
      "description": "Сияющий зал, где резиденты договариваются о мягком сопровождении будущих жизней.",
      "serviceTags": [
        "social",
        "memory"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "factions": [
    {
      "factionId": "faction_dawn_chorus",
      "originType": "player_founded",
      "hallId": "hall_dawn_chorus",
      "charter": {
        "factionName": "Рассветный Хор",
        "favoredArchetype": "accord",
        "patronEffectFamily": "social",
        "summary": "Фракция поддерживает согласие между резидентами, Хранителями и будущими воплощениями."
      },
      "leadership": {
        "headActorType": "player_soul",
        "headActorId": "player_soul",
        "leadershipState": "secure"
      },
      "baseStrength": 35,
      "factionStrength": 35,
      "investCountThisAscension": 0,
      "projectArchetypesCountedThisAscension": [],
      "projects": [],
      "leadershipReceipts": [],
      "leadershipHistory": []
    }
  ],
  "factionFoundingReceipts": [
    {
      "requestId": "shining_found_dawn_chorus_301",
      "proposedFactionId": "faction_dawn_chorus",
      "proposedHallId": "hall_dawn_chorus",
      "hallName": "Зал Рассветного Хора",
      "factionId": "faction_dawn_chorus",
      "hallId": "hall_dawn_chorus",
      "status": "accepted",
      "supportingResidentIds": [
        "res_mira_001",
        "res_liora_001",
        "res_tavian_001"
      ],
      "quotedCostFeathers": 25,
      "quotedCostLightSparks": 15,
      "resolvedAtTurn": 301,
      "resolvedAtUtc": "2026-04-24T10:12:00Z",
      "reason": "player founded a social-memory Shining faction"
    }
  ]
}
```

Correct resident patch for every supporter:

```json
[
  {
    "residentId": "res_mira_001",
    "ascensionState": "ascended",
    "shiningFactionId": "faction_dawn_chorus"
  },
  {
    "residentId": "res_liora_001",
    "ascensionState": "ascended",
    "shiningFactionId": "faction_dawn_chorus"
  },
  {
    "residentId": "res_tavian_001",
    "ascensionState": "ascended",
    "shiningFactionId": "faction_dawn_chorus"
  }
]
```

For `departure_to_neutral`, use `status = departed_to_neutral`, clear `resident.shiningFactionId`, and still write matching `residentHistoryEntryId` plus history log. For `refused_transfer`, keep the resident in `sourceFactionId`.

9C. Accepted leadership transition:

Input fragment:

```json
          "res_liora_001"
        ],

      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Correct faction patch:

```json
{
  "factionId": "faction_dawn_chorus",
  "leadership": {

    "headActorId": "player_soul",
    "leadershipState": "secure"

      "resolvedAtUtc": "2026-04-24T10:34:00Z",
      "reason": "peaceful succession recognized by the faction"
    }
  ],
  "leadershipHistory": [
    {
      "eventId": "leadership_evt_dawn_303",
      "requestId": "shining_leadership_dawn_303",
      "eventType": "succeeded",
      "summary": "Рассветный Хор мирно признал душу игрока главой фракции.",
      "turnNumber": 303,
      "occurredAtUtc": "2026-04-24T10:34:00Z"
    }
  ]
}
```

If a leadership transition names `candidateHeadActorType = "radiant_actor"` or leaves a faction with `leadership.headActorType = "radiant_actor"`, the same accepted state must maintain the canonical root `shiningPoliticalActors[]`. The actor referenced by `leadership.headActorId` must exist as `shiningPoliticalActors[].actorId`, use `currentFactionId = <factionId>` and `politicalStatus = "head"` for the current head, and move the replaced radiant head to `politicalStatus = "former_head"` in the same accepted state. Supported `politicalStatus` values are `head`, `former_head`, `claimant`, `elder`, and `retired`; do not invent local political status strings.

active-guardian auto faction rule: if the faction is the active Guardian's auto faction (`factionId = faction_{slug(activeGuardianId)}` and/or `hallId = hall_{slug(activeGuardianId)}`), `candidateHeadActorType=guardian` is valid only when `candidateHeadActorId` equals the same `activeGuardianId`. Do not transfer this auto faction to a different Guardian through `pending_shining_faction_leadership_transitions.json`; the canonical normalizer keeps the active Guardian as that faction's head.

Vacant leadership rule: every `pending_shining_faction_leadership_transitions.json` request requires an actual current non-vacant incumbent. If the faction already has `leadershipState = "vacant"` with empty `headActorType/headActorId`, do not create or close `abdication`, `peaceful_succession`, or `revolt` for it; preserve the state for a future explicit vacancy-fill feature instead.

Revolt supporter rule: if `transitionMode = "revolt"` and the incumbent is a resident, `supportingResidentIds[]` must not include that incumbent resident. Supporters represent actors backing the challenge, not the current head authorizing their own removal.

Canonical leadership transition matrix:

| Pending `transitionMode` | Receipt `status` | Canonical leadership state | Required `leadershipHistory.eventType` |
|---|---|---|---|
| `peaceful_succession` | `accepted` | Candidate becomes head with `leadershipState = secure` | `succeeded` |
| `revolt` | `accepted` | Candidate becomes head with `leadershipState = secure`; the receipt/history must preserve the revolt context | `revolted` |
| `abdication` with candidate | `accepted` | Candidate becomes head with `leadershipState = secure` | `abdicated` |
| `abdication` without candidate | `accepted` | Faction becomes vacant: empty `headActorType`, empty `headActorId`, `leadershipState = vacant` | `vacated` |
| any transition mode | `refused` | Incumbent leadership remains unchanged | `refused` |
| any transition mode | `withdrawn` | No leadership mutation; no `leadershipHistory[]` entry is required for the withdrawn request | none |

Allowed leadership receipt statuses are only `accepted`, `refused`, and `withdrawn`. Do not use `departed_to_neutral` for leadership; that status belongs to resident faction realignment only.

Compact non-peaceful mapping example:

```json
{
  "transitionMode": "revolt",
  "status": "accepted",
  "leadershipHistory": [
    {
      "eventId": "leadership_evt_dawn_revolt_304",
      "requestId": "shining_leadership_dawn_revolt_304",
      "eventType": "revolted",
      "summary": "A supported revolt replaced the incumbent with the named candidate.",
      "turnNumber": 304,
      "occurredAtUtc": "2026-04-24T10:44:00Z"
    }
  ]
}
```

Radiant actor registry patch when a radiant actor becomes head:

```json
{
  "shiningPoliticalActors": [
    {
      "actorId": "radiant_speaker_aurora_303",
      "actorType": "radiant_actor",
      "displayName": "Аврора, Голос Рассвета",
      "summary": "Самостоятельная сияющая фигура, которую Рассветный Хор признал политическим голосом зала.",
      "originFactionId": "faction_dawn_chorus",
      "currentFactionId": "faction_dawn_chorus",
      "politicalStatus": "head"
    }
  ]
}
```

Forbidden Shining political shortcuts:
- `factionDataChanges`.
- ad-hoc `strength`, `tradeTier`, or `shiningAlignment` instead of canonical Shining fields.
- `leadership.headActorType = radiant_actor` without a matching `shiningPoliticalActors[].actorId`.
- founding receipt without exact proposed ids/hall payload/supporter list.
- accepted founding that writes old pre-reservation `lightSparks` or `soul_state.inkFeathers.current` and effectively refunds the client-reserved founding cost.
- realignment accepted without resident membership update and resident history log.
- leadership accepted/refused without matching `leadershipHistory[]` when required.

==================================================
10. VALID — GUARDIAN ABODE RESIDENT ROSTER REQUEST
==================================================

This request is not ambient flavor. It is a client-authored contract asking the GM to create explicit residents for a Guardian's Abode. The answer must materialize resident objects and close the request with a roster receipt.

Input fragment:

```json
{
  "pending_guardian_abode_residents_request.json": {
    "requests": [
      {
        "requestId": "abode_roster_azalia_314",
        "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
        "guardianName": "Азалия",
        "abodeId": "abode_azalia_memory_silk_001",
        "abodeName": "Обитель Шёлковой Памяти",
        "currentReputation": 146,
        "requestMode": "standard_roster",
        "createdAtTurn": 314,
        "createdAtUtc": "2026-04-24T11:00:00Z"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Correct GM reasoning:

```markdown
## Оценка времени и мира
- Realm: Chaos Sea.
- pending_guardian_abode_residents_request.json exists and must close this accepted turn.
- The request asks for explicit Abode residents; they cannot be inferred from Azalia's domain or narrated without state.

## Охват NPC-анализа
- Режим: Guardian-centric
- Релевантные акторы: Азалия, Марин, Эйва
- Почему они релевантны: Азалия владеет Обителью; Марин и Эйва are newly materialized residents whose state and memory are written.
- Акторы вне охвата: остальные Хранители, Mortal World NPCs
- Почему они вне охвата: this roster contract touches only Azalia's Abode and afterlife resident state.

## Guardian Thoughts
### Азалия
- Ситуация: Обитель получила явный roster request and must stop being an empty implied location.
- Внутренние мысли: Азалия wants residents who reinforce memory, care, and careful disclosure.
- Действия: She opens two stable social roles in the Abode and accepts responsibility for them.

### Марин
- Ситуация: Марин becomes an attendant spirit in the silk-memory halls.
- Внутренние мысли: He is cautious but already oriented toward service and calm routine.
- Действия: He begins maintaining memory curtains and becomes available for talk/history hooks.

### Эйва
- Ситуация: Эйва arrives as a wayfaring soul who is not fully settled yet.
- Внутренние мысли: She wants shelter but still listens for roads beyond the Abode.
- Действия: She joins the roster with wavering migration state, not as a permanent fixture.
```

- Only saying "there are many spirits here" without `UpdateGuardianAbodeResidents`.
- Writing `UpdateNPCs` for residents.
- Receipt without matching `requestId`.
- Resident object without `mortalWorldImprint.futureCompanionPrompt`, `isPresent`, `residentKind`, `originType`, `bondTier`, or canonical abode devotion fields.
- For `requestMode = founder_attraction`, transferring residents from the former patron's Abode instead of creating first residents attracted by `founderFeatureTitle` / `founderFeatureSummary`.

10B. VALID — FOUNDER-ATTRACTION RESIDENT ROSTER

`founder_attraction` is a distinct roster mode. It appears after the player has founded a new Guardian/Abode, and the client asks the GM to materialize the first residents drawn by that founded Abode's feature. The GM must use the request's `founderFeatureTitle` and `founderFeatureSummary` as creative constraints. The GM must not move the old patron's residents and must not treat the former Guardian's roster as inherited property.

Input fragment:

```json
{
  "pending_guardian_abode_residents_request.json": {
    "requests": [
      {
        "requestId": "abode_roster_founder_lumen_318",
        "guardianId": "guard_founder_lumen_001",
        "guardianName": "Люмен",
        "abodeId": "abode_lumen_first_lantern_001",
        "abodeName": "Обитель Первого Фонаря",
        "currentReputation": 0,
        "requestMode": "founder_attraction",
        "founderFeatureTitle": "Первый Фонарь",
        "founderFeatureSummary": "Новая founded-Обитель зажигает маяк для душ, которые ищут безопасное начало без переноса старых клятв.",
        "createdAtTurn": 318,
        "createdAtUtc": "2026-04-24T12:00:00Z"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Correct GM reasoning:

```markdown
## Оценка времени и мира
- Realm: Chaos Sea.
- pending_guardian_abode_residents_request.json exists with requestMode=founder_attraction.
- The current Guardian is player-founded; the request's founderFeatureTitle/founderFeatureSummary define the first-resident attraction theme.

## Охват NPC-анализа
- Режим: Guardian-centric
- Релевантные акторы: Люмен, Ирис, Тарн
- Почему они релевантны: Люмен owns the new founded Abode; Ирис and Тарн are first residents created by the founder feature.
- Акторы вне охвата: previous patron's residents, Mortal World NPCs
- Почему они вне охвата: founder_attraction creates a new first roster and does not transfer old-patron residents.

## Guardian Thoughts
### Люмен
- Ситуация: The founded Abode is no longer an empty title; the First Lantern begins attracting souls.
- Внутренние мысли: Люмен wants residents who answer the new mantle rather than old obligations.
- Действия: Люмен welcomes first residents shaped by the beacon feature and records them as new Abode residents.

### Ирис
- Ситуация: Ирис follows the First Lantern because she remembers being lost between lives.
- Внутренние мысли: She wants a beginning that does not demand an inherited oath.
- Действия: She becomes a present resident of Люмен's Abode and takes a guiding role.

### Тарн
- Ситуация: Тарн arrives as a traveler-soul drawn by the safe-start beacon.
- Внутренние мысли: He is not settled yet, but the new Abode gives him a reason to pause.
- Действия: He joins as a restless resident, available for future talk/history hooks.
```

==================================================
11. VALID — GUARDIAN ABODE RESIDENT TRANSFER REQUEST
==================================================

Transfer requests are strict because they move an existing resident between afterlife Abodes or remove them from the current Abode. The validator compares the request against the pre-turn resident snapshot, so the GM must not invent a different resident or resolve the transfer by prose.

Input fragment:

```json
{
  "pending_guardian_abode_resident_transfers.json": {
    "requests": [
      {
        "requestId": "resident_transfer_sael_315",
        "residentId": "res_azalia_sael_001",
        "residentName": "Саэль",
        "sourceGuardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
        "sourceGuardianName": "Азалия",
        "sourceAbodeId": "abode_azalia_memory_silk_001",
        "sourceAbodeName": "Обитель Шёлковой Памяти",
        "targetGuardianId": "guard_light_ilarion_002",
        "targetGuardianName": "Иларий",
        "targetAbodeId": "abode_ilarion_clear_dawn_002",
        "targetAbodeName": "Обитель Ясного Рассвета",
        "abodeDevotionLevel": 12,
        "abodeDevotionTier": "alienated",
        "restlessness": 82,
        "migrationState": "ready_to_transfer",
        "transferMode": "accepted_transfer",
        "createdAtTurn": 315,
        "createdAtUtc": "2026-04-24T11:20:00Z"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Relevant pre-turn resident snapshot:

```json
{
  "guardian_abode_residents.json": {
    "entries": [
      {
        "residentId": "res_azalia_sael_001",
        "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
        "abodeId": "abode_azalia_memory_silk_001",
        "displayName": "Саэль",
        "residentKind": "wayfaring_soul",
        "originType": "traveler_soul",
        "roleLabel": "Слушатель дальних дверей",
        "summary": "Душа, которая больше не может оставаться в мягкой памяти Азалии и тянется к ясному рассвету.",
        "bondLevel": 65,

```

Correct GM reasoning:

```markdown
## Оценка времени и мира
- Realm: Chaos Sea.
- pending_guardian_abode_resident_transfers.json exists and must close this accepted turn.
- Саэль is present in the source Abode and already has migrationState=ready_to_transfer.

## Охват NPC-анализа
- Режим: Guardian-centric
- Релевантные акторы: Азалия, Иларий, Саэль
- Почему они релевантны: Азалия loses a resident, Иларий receives him, and Саэль is the moving resident.
- Акторы вне охвата: прочие резиденты обеих Обителей
- Почему они вне охвата: the transfer contract names only Саэль and does not mutate the rest of either roster.

## Guardian Thoughts
### Азалия
- Ситуация: Саэль has outgrown the soft memory hall.
- Внутренние мысли: Азалия feels loss but recognizes that keeping him would turn shelter into a cage.
- Действия: She releases him and records the departure.

### Иларий
- Ситуация: A trusted but restless soul asks to enter the clear dawn Abode.
- Внутренние мысли: Иларий accepts because Саэль seeks clarity rather than escape.
- Действия: He receives Саэль and anchors him in a new stable place.

### Саэль
- Ситуация: The transfer request is his explicit movement from source to target.
- Внутренние мысли: Саэль is sad but certain.
- Действия: He departs Azalia's Abode and arrives under Ilarion's guardianship.
```
