Other transfer modes:
- `refused_transfer`: keep the resident present in the source Abode, do not create a target arrival, write `status = refused`, `transferMode = refused_transfer`, one refusal/departure history entry, and a matching `departureHistoryEntryId`.
- `departure_only`: remove the resident from source presence or set the same resident `isPresent = false`, do not write target guardian/abode fields, write `status = departed_only`, `transferMode = departure_only`, one departure history entry, and a matching `departureHistoryEntryId`.
- For every mode, use the same `requestId` and `residentId` from the pending contract. Never create a new resident identity to represent the transfer.

Invalid transfer shortcuts:
- Receipt without history references.
- `accepted_transfer` that leaves the resident present in the source Abode.
- `departure_only` with target guardian/abode or competition metadata.
- `refused_transfer` that materializes a target arrival anyway.
- Using Shining faction realignment receipts for Guardian Abode resident transfer. These are separate systems.

==================================================
12. VALID — GUARDIAN SOCIAL INTERACTION REQUEST
==================================================

Guardian social requests are stricter than ordinary freeform Guardian scenes. If `pending_guardian_social_interactions.json` exists, every request must close through `guardianSocialJournalUpdates` with matching closure metadata.

Input fragment:

```json
{
  "pending_guardian_social_interactions.json": {
    "requests": [
      {
        "requestId": "guardian_social_azalia_lore_316",
        "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
        "guardianName": "Азалия",
        "interactionType": "lore",
        "createdAtTurn": 316,
        "createdAtUtc": "2026-04-24T11:40:00Z"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Correct GM reasoning:

```markdown
## Оценка времени и мира
- Realm: Chaos Sea.
- pending_guardian_social_interactions.json exists; this is a strict closure request, not optional memory flavor.
- interactionType=lore, so accepted closure should use a lore responseMode or a canonical refusal if Azalia refuses.

## Охват NPC-анализа
- Режим: Guardian-centric
- Релевантные акторы: Азалия
- Почему они релевантны: the request names Azalia and the output writes guardianSocialJournalUpdates for her.
- Акторы вне охвата: other Guardians, residents, Shining factions
- Почему они вне охвата: no state or journal entry for them is changed.

## Guardian Thoughts
### Азалия
- Ситуация: The soul asks for lore from Azalia.
- Внутренние мысли: She can reveal one safe fragment without exposing the deeper forbidden layer.
- Действия: She accepts the request, gives a bounded lore answer, and records the social memory entry.
```

Correct response/state fragment:

```json
{
  "guardianSocialJournalUpdates": [
    {
      "entryId": "guardian_social_entry_azalia_lore_316",
      "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
      "requestId": "guardian_social_azalia_lore_316",
      "interactionType": "lore",
      "status": "accepted",
      "responseMode": "lore_revealed",
      "title": "Азалия раскрыла шёлковую клятву",
      "summary": "Азалия объяснила, что её Обитель связывает память не запретом, а бережным темпом раскрытия.",
      "eventType": "lore",
      "consequence": "soul gains a safer interpretation of Azalia's domain",
      "attitude": "guarded_trust",
      "intent": "reveal one safe layer and withhold dangerous details",
      "tags": [
        "guardian_social",
        "lore"
      ],
      "turn": 316,
      "timestamp": "2026-04-24T11:44:00Z"
    }
  ],
  "guardianThoughtJournalUpdates": [
    {
      "entryId": "guardian_thought_azalia_lore_316",
      "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
      "title": "Граница безопасного раскрытия",
      "summary": "Азалия решила, что душа заслужила один слой истины, но ещё не готова к запретному ядру её памяти.",
      "eventType": "inner_stance",
      "consequence": "future lore requests can build on this boundary",
      "attitude": "careful",
      "intent": "protect the soul from violent memory recovery",
      "tags": [
        "thought",
        "lore_boundary"
      ],
      "turn": 316,
      "timestamp": "2026-04-24T11:44:00Z"
    }
  ]
}
```

Invalid guardian social shortcuts:
- Missing `requestId`, `guardianId`, `interactionType`, or `status` in the journal entry.
- `interactionType` other than `talk` or `lore`.
- `responseMode` outside `talk_scene`, `lore_revealed`, `lore_refused`, `warning`, `refusal`, `trust_shift`, `attitude_shift`.
- Writing only `guardianThoughtJournalUpdates`; the strict closure must be in `guardianSocialJournalUpdates`.

==================================================
13. VALID — PLAYER-FOUNDED GUARDIAN FOUNDATION
==================================================

`pending_player_guardian_foundation.json` is a late Chaos Sea ritual after Shining return. The player remains `player_soul`; the GM creates a new Guardian mantle as a separate guardian actor. This is not ascension, not New Game+, and not rewriting the soul into an NPC.

Runtime preconditions before the GM may close this contract:
- current realm is exactly `Chaos Sea`;
- `soul_state.soulName` / `founderSoulName` is non-empty;
- `shining_abode_state.json.availability = sealed_until_next_ascension`;
- `preparedIncarnationPackage` is null/absent;
- no active, malformed, wrong-reason, or otherwise blocking `afterlife_return_guard.json`; no malformed/competing foundation request; and no existing `playerFoundedGuardianId` / already founded Guardian for this soul;
- pending request echoes `sourceShiningAvailability = sealed_until_next_ascension`.

Input fragment:

```json
{
  "pending_player_guardian_foundation.json": {
    "requestId": "foundation_tide_memory_317",
    "mode": "player_founded_guardian",
    "founderSoulName": "Станислав",
    "previousGuardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
    "previousGuardianName": "Азалия",
    "sourceShiningAvailability": "sealed_until_next_ascension",
    "proposedDisplayName": "Мантия Прилива Памяти",
    "mantleSummary": "Новая Хранительская мантия, основанная душой игрока после возвращения из Сияющей Обители.",
    "mantleCreed": "Память должна вести к новой жизни, а не удерживать её в клетке.",
    "appearanceMotifs": [
      "чернильные перья",
      "серебряный прилив",
      "порог рассвета"
    ],
    "dominantAspect": "memory",
    "createdAtTurn": 317,
    "createdAtUtc": "2026-04-24T12:00:00Z"
  }
}
```

Correct GM reasoning:

```markdown
## Оценка времени и мира
- Realm: Chaos Sea.
- pending_player_guardian_foundation.json exists and is valid only in Chaos Sea.
- This turn creates a new Guardian actor; it does not delete Azalia, does not move the player out of player_soul identity, and does not trigger incarnation.

## Охват NPC-анализа
- Режим: Guardian-centric
- Релевантные акторы: Азалия, Мантия Прилива Памяти
- Почему они релевантны: Azalia becomes former_patron; the new mantle is created, becomes activeGuardian, and receives a new Abode.
- Акторы вне охвата: other Guardians, Shining factions, Mortal World NPCs
- Почему они вне охвата: the foundation contract names only the previous patron and the new player-founded mantle.

## Guardian Thoughts
### Азалия
- Ситуация: The soul no longer needs Azalia as active patron, but she must remain in guardians[].
- Внутренние мысли: Azalia accepts becoming former_patron rather than being erased or demoted into an NPC.
- Действия: She steps aside and remains a narrative relationship hook.

### Мантия Прилива Памяти
- Ситуация: The player's ascended return creates a new Guardian mantle.
- Внутренние мысли: The mantle is loyal to the founding soul but distinct from the soul's player identity.
- Действия: It materializes as a new guardian actor, opens its Abode, and becomes activeGuardian.
```

```json
        "mood": {
          "current": "solemn",
          "intensity": 70,
          "reason": "Новая мантия только что учреждена душой игрока.",
          "since": 317
        },
        "musings": []
      }
    }
  ],
  "activeGuardian": {
    "guardianId": "guard_player_tide_memory_317"
  },
  "chaosSeaNavigation": {
    "currentAbodeId": "abode_player_tide_memory_317",
    "discoveredAbodes": [
      "abode_azalia_memory_silk_001",
      "abode_player_tide_memory_317"
    ]
  },
  "playerGuardianFoundationHistory": [
    {
      "requestId": "foundation_tide_memory_317",
      "guardianId": "guard_player_tide_memory_317",
      "guardianDisplayName": "Мантия Прилива Памяти",
      "founderSoulName": "Станислав",
      "formerPatronGuardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
      "formerPatronGuardianName": "Азалия",
      "foundationSource": "shining_return",
      "resolvedAtTurn": 317,
      "resolvedAtUtc": "2026-04-24T12:08:00Z"
    }
  ],
  "metaStateUpdates": {
    "playerFoundedGuardianId": "guard_player_tide_memory_317",
    "playerGuardianFoundationStatus": "founded"
  }
}
```

Required final-state facts:
- Keep the previous Guardian in `guardians[]`; do not delete Azalia.
- Mark the previous Guardian's `relationshipData.guardianRoleToPlayer = former_patron`.
- If the GM authors a `guardians` canonical array directly, it must contain full Guardian objects copied from state plus intended changes, not partial patch objects.
- The new player-founded Guardian must carry `originType = player_founded_ascended_soul`, `founderSoulName`, `founderLoyaltyTier = soulbound`, `foundationSource = shining_return`, `foundationRequestId`, `founderBonuses.extraGachaChargesPerReturn = 1`, and `founderAbodeFeatures.residentAttractionMode = founder_call`.

Invalid foundation shortcuts:
- Rewriting `soul_state.soulName` into a Guardian actor.
- Deleting or replacing the previous active Guardian.
- Creating the new mantle without `UpdateGuardians.create`.
- Forgetting `playerGuardianFoundationHistory`.
- Forgetting `soul_state.playerFoundedGuardianId` or `playerGuardianFoundationStatus = founded`.
- Automatically moving old Abode residents into the new founded Abode.

==================================================
14. VALID — SHINING CORE ACTION CONTRACTS
==================================================

`pending_shining_abode_actions.json` is the canonical client-authored contract for active Shining Abode actions. It is not optional flavor. If the file exists, the GM must close exactly the pending request through `game_state/meta/shining_abode_state.json.coreActionReceipts[]` and the exact state mutation required by that `actionType`.

Queue and mode rules:
- The file root is always `requests[]`; it contains exactly one active Shining core request, not a GM-managed queue. Do not write a singular root request property and do not invent a second simultaneous Shining core request.
- Ordinary active Shining requires `currentRealm = Shining Abode`, `shining_abode_state.json.availability = active`, and `preparedIncarnationPackage = null`/absent. `sealed_until_next_ascension` is accepted persisted state, but it is not ordinary active Shining gameplay and must not process core/trade/political actions.
- Pending-bootstrap is derived from non-null valid `preparedIncarnationPackage`; in that mode the GM preserves the package and does not process ordinary Shining core actions.
- Cost fields in receipts identify the accepted request, but resource spends, Radiance XP, strength changes, gate invalidation, and package creation must also be reflected through canonical Shining/Soul state deltas.
- Blessing-card rarity order is `common -> uncommon -> rare -> epic -> legendary -> radiant`; project-card bonuses upgrade only one step on that ladder, so a `rare` project card becomes `epic`, not immediately `radiant`.
- Blessing-card token fields are strict in both `gates.*BlessingCards[]` and `preparedIncarnationPackage.selectedCards[]`: `sourceType = head | project | resident_descent`; `effectFamily = lore | social | resource | memory | descent | survival | relic | route`; `rarity = common | uncommon | rare | epic | legendary | radiant`.
- Forge blessing rerolls are client-side `pendingShiningBlessingEffects.relicRefinementEntitlements`: the player preview shows before/after reroll counts, cancel preserves the entitlement, and the client consumes rerolls only after final forge-request confirmation.

Client-owned local Gates operations:
- Selecting a blessing card, deselecting it, or rerolling the open Gates draft is not a GM turn.
- These operations create no `pending_shining_abode_actions.json`, no `coreActionReceipts[]`, no `TriggerIncarnation`, and no accepted-turn receipt.
- They only mutate local `gates` draft fields: `selectedBlessingCardIds`, `shownBlessingCardIds`, `availableBlessingCards`, `rerollsRemaining`, and `nextCandidateCursor`.
- The GM reads those fields as current context on a later real turn. Do not invent a receipt because a card was selected locally.
- Local selection/reroll is blocked if the draft is stale, a package is already prepared, or an unresolved Shining core pending request exists.

Shining resident normalizer side effects:
- A resident is Shining-aligned only when `ascensionState = "ascended"` and `shiningFactionId` points to an existing Shining faction.
- If not ascended, runtime normalizes the resident to `ascensionState = "remained_in_chaos_sea"`, clears `shiningFactionId` and `residentRole`, sets `factionLoyaltyLevel = 0`, `factionLoyaltyTier = "alienated"`, `factionRestlessness = 0`, and `factionRealignmentState = "settled"`.
- If ascended but `shiningFactionId` is missing or points to a non-existing faction, runtime preserves `ascensionState = "ascended"` and treats the resident as ascended but unaffiliated: it clears `shiningFactionId` and `residentRole`, sets `factionLoyaltyLevel = 0`, `factionLoyaltyTier = "alienated"`, `factionRestlessness = 0`, and `factionRealignmentState = "settled"`.
- If ascended and faction exists, runtime derives/validates `residentRole`, `factionLoyaltyLevel`, `factionLoyaltyTier`, `factionRestlessness`, and `factionRealignmentState`. Do not use non-canonical `shiningAlignment`.

Canonical Shining owner-state invariants:
- `halls[].hallId`, `factions[].factionId`, `shiningPoliticalActors[].actorId`, and every `projects[].projectId` across all factions must be unique. Duplicate ids make runtime helpers choose the first match and are invalid.
- Supported completed projects are globally capped by Radiance tier: tier `0..1` allows `1`, tier `2..3` allows `2`, tier `4` allows `3`. If more completed projects have `isSupported = true`, preserve for repair or close an explicit `unsupport_project`; do not keep the over-cap state.
- `gachaSystem.currentReturnCycleId` may be empty only before the first Shining return-cycle sync. While it is empty, `gachaSystem.chargesUsedThisReturn` must be `0`; positive used charges without a cycle id are stale state.

Canonical Shining vocabulary tables:

| Field | Allowed values | Use |
|---|---|---|
| `leadershipState` | `secure`, `contested`, `vacant` | Faction leadership status; `vacant` only when no head remains. |
| faction `originType` | `ascended_guardian`, `native_radiant`, `player_founded` | Where a Shining faction came from. |
| `residentRole` | `archive_support`, `forge_support`, `social_support`, `resource_support`, `descent_support` | Ascended resident's faction role and blessing/trade contribution. |
| `factionLoyaltyTier` | `alienated`, `uncertain`, `attached`, `devoted`, `steadfast` | Derived from resident loyalty/restlessness; do not invent prose tiers. |
| `factionRealignmentState` | `settled`, `wavering`, `restless`, `considering_realignment`, `ready_to_realign` | Whether the resident is stable or ready to change faction. |
| `projectArchetype` | `revelation`, `accord`, `provision`, `remembrance`, `refinement`, `passage`, `warding`, `subversion` | Project cost/effect classification. |

Canonical Abode resident disposition/devotion/migration tables:

| Field | Allowed values / mapping |
|---|---|
| `abodeDisposition.powerSensitivity` | `low`, `medium`, `high` |
| `abodeDisposition.migrationDisposition` | `rooted`, `selective`, `opportunistic`, `wandering` |
| `abodeDisposition.communalOrientation` | `low`, `medium`, `high` |
| `abodeDisposition.stabilityNeed` | `low`, `medium`, `high` |
| `abodeDevotionTier` | `alienated` for devotion `0..19`, `uncertain` for `20..39`, `attached` for `40..59`, `devoted` for `60..79`, `steadfast` for `80..100`; `guest` is invalid. |
| `migrationState` | `ready_to_transfer` when devotion `<=15` and restlessness `>=70`; `considering_departure` when devotion `<=30` and restlessness `>=55`; `restless` when devotion `<=45` or restlessness `>=45`; `wavering` when devotion `<=60` or restlessness `>=30`; otherwise `settled`. |

Accepted Shining core actions are strict diff contracts: mutate only the canonical state surfaces projected by that `actionType` plus the matching `coreActionReceipts[]` entry. `refused` and `withdrawn` outcomes close only through the receipt and must leave canonical Shining, Soul, and resident state unchanged. Supported receipt statuses are only `accepted`, `refused`, and `withdrawn`.

The client preview may show a `projectedStateFragment` containing sanitized `beforeFullShiningRoot`, `afterFullShiningRoot`, and focused `gates`/faction/project fragments. Forge previews can also show sanitized `beforeFullSoulRoot`/`afterFullSoulRoot`; Shining gacha previews can show `gachaAccounting`, `expectedGachaHistoryEntryShape`, and `oneNewRelicEvidence`. Treat these as audit aids only: they are not GM output files and not new response fields. The accepted response still persists the full canonical `game_state/meta/shining_abode_state.json` root, `game_state/meta/soul_state.json` when listed in `changedSurfaces`, plus `coreActionReceipts[]`; hidden blessing `effectPayload` keys must not be copied from previews.

If the same accepted turn also closes a Shining political/trade/resident pending contract, the final state is the composition of all accepted closures. For example, accepted faction founding first materializes the hall/faction and supporter resident alignment, then `open_gates` builds its draft from that updated Shining state; both `factionFoundingReceipts[]` and `coreActionReceipts[]` must be present.

The strict Shining closure validator uses pre-turn `game_state/control/pending_turn_snapshot` roots for `game_state/meta/shining_abode_state.json`, `game_state/meta/guardian_abode_residents.json`, and `game_state/meta/soul_state.json`. It accepts only the canonical projected diff for the Shining core request plus other explicitly closed afterlife contracts in the same accepted response. Unrelated edits fail as `shining_closure_unexpected_shining_state_diff`, `shining_closure_unexpected_resident_state_diff`, or `shining_closure_unexpected_soul_state_diff`; missing baseline files fail as `shining_closure_missing_pre_turn_shining_state`, `shining_closure_missing_pre_turn_resident_state`, or `shining_closure_missing_pre_turn_soul_state`. A legal concurrent Soul delta must be proved by its own contract, for example an `ABODE_OFFERING` receipt/state change, not by silently changing `soul_state.json` during Shining closure.

Supported `actionType` values:

```text
discover_native_faction
invest_in_faction
complete_project
support_project
unsupport_project
retire_project
open_gates
prepare_incarnation_package
pull_relic_gacha
forge_relic.reshape
forge_relic.retune_property
forge_relic.strengthen_band
forge_relic.stabilize_echo
forge_relic.uplift_rarity
```

Common accepted receipt fields for every Shining core action:

```json
{
  "requestId": "exact requestId from pending_shining_abode_actions.json",
  "actionType": "exact actionType from request",
  "status": "accepted",
  "factionId": "same factionId as request, or empty string when request has none",
  "projectId": "same projectId as request; for complete_project this may be the newly created projectId",
  "relicId": "same relicId as request, or for pull_relic_gacha the newly materialized result relicId",
  "returnCycleId": "same returnCycleId as request, or empty string",
  "targetFormTag": "same targetFormTag as request, or empty string",
  "propertyIndex": -1,
  "quotedCostFeathers": 0,
  "quotedCostLightSparks": 0,
  "selectedCardIds": [],
  "newResidentIds": [],
  "seededProjectIds": [],
  "generatedDraftVersion": 0,
  "resolvedAtTurn": 321,
  "resolvedAtUtc": "2026-04-24T13:00:00Z",
  "reason": "short machine-readable reason"
}
```

Important receipt rules:
- Always keep `requestId`, `actionType`, target ids, `selectedCardIds`, `returnCycleId`, `targetFormTag`, and `propertyIndex` synchronized with the pending request. Exception: `pull_relic_gacha` introduces a new non-empty result `relicId` in the receipt because the pending request cannot know that id in advance.
- Always include `quotedCostFeathers` and `quotedCostLightSparks`, exactly copied from the pending request even when the value is `0`.
- Always include `selectedCardIds`, `newResidentIds`, and `seededProjectIds`; use empty arrays when the action does not use them.
- Always include positive `resolvedAtTurn` and non-empty ISO `resolvedAtUtc`.
- `status = refused` or `withdrawn` closes the request but must not apply the accepted state mutation.

14A. Discovery and faction/project actions:

```json
{
  "pending_shining_abode_actions.json": {
    "requests": [
      {
        "requestId": "shine_discover_native_321",
        "actionType": "discover_native_faction",
        "radianceTierAtRequest": 2,
        "quotedCostFeathers": 25,
        "quotedCostLightSparks": 20,
        "sourceDraftVersion": 0,
        "selectedCardIds": [],
        "createdAtTurn": 321,
        "createdAtUtc": "2026-04-24T13:00:00Z"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Accepted `discover_native_faction` must:
- spend exactly `quotedCostFeathers` from `soul_state.json.inkFeathers`;
- spend exactly `quotedCostLightSparks` from `shining_abode_state.json.lightSparks`;
- add exactly `+20` Radiance XP and recalculate Radiance tier canonically;
- materialize one new hall in `halls[]`; its `hallId` must not exist in pre-turn `shining_abode_state.halls[]`;
- materialize one new faction with `originType = native_radiant` and `hallId` matching the receipt; its `factionId` must not exist in pre-turn `shining_abode_state.factions[]`;
- materialize `2..4` new ascended residents in `guardian_abode_residents.json`;
- create exactly `2` seeded completed projects inside the new faction; every `projectId` must be new across all pre-turn Shining faction projects;
- write a receipt whose `hallId`, `resolvedFactionId`, `newResidentIds[]`, and `seededProjectIds[]` point to those materialized objects;
- keep the accepted diff constrained to discovery surfaces: do not change pre-existing halls, factions/projects, residents, political actors, or unrelated Soul state.

Legacy `pendingNativeFactionDiscovery` closure:
- If pre-turn `shining_abode_state.json.pendingNativeFactionDiscovery` is non-null and there is no matching `pending_shining_abode_actions.json` request, treat it as the legacy state-local version of `discover_native_faction`.
- Use `pendingNativeFactionDiscovery.requestId` as the receipt `requestId`; use `actionType = "discover_native_faction"`.
- Materialize the same hall/native faction/residents/projects/Radiance outcome as above.
- Spend `pendingNativeFactionDiscovery.costFeathers` from `soul_state.json.inkFeathers`.
- Preserve current `shining_abode_state.json.lightSparks`; the legacy queue already reserved `pendingNativeFactionDiscovery.costLightSparks`, so do not subtract Light Sparks a second time.
- Legacy receipt cost audit is intentionally asymmetric: write `quotedCostFeathers = pendingNativeFactionDiscovery.costFeathers`, but write `quotedCostLightSparks = 0` because Light Sparks were reserved before GM closure.
- Set `shining_abode_state.json.pendingNativeFactionDiscovery = null` in the accepted state.
- Do not create or echo a duplicate `pending_shining_abode_actions.json` request for this legacy payload.
- Do not use the legacy closure to edit unrelated existing Shining state; only the newly discovered hall/faction/residents/projects, Radiance, Soul Feather debit, pending slot, and receipt may change.

Correct legacy `pendingNativeFactionDiscovery` receipt cost audit:


For these action types, accepted state must equal the canonical helper projection from pre-turn state:

```text
invest_in_faction
  Request requires: factionId, quotedCostFeathers = 10, quotedCostLightSparks = 5.
  State result: spend exact costs, increment faction.investCountThisAscension, recompute factionStrength, mark open gates stale if needed.
  Receipt target: factionId only; projectId/relicId/returnCycleId empty.

complete_project
  Request requires: factionId, projectDraft, quoted costs from the canonical quote.
  State result: spend exact costs, append one completed project generated from projectDraft, possibly add Radiance XP for a newly counted archetype, recompute factionStrength, mark gates stale.
  Favored archetype is cost-only: if projectDraft.projectArchetype matches faction.charter.favoredArchetype, the quote is 5 Feathers and 5 Light Sparks cheaper; strengthReward is still tier-only (tier 1 = 8, tier 2 = 12, tier 3 = 16).
  Receipt target: factionId and non-empty projectId of the completed project.

support_project
  Request requires: factionId and projectId of an existing completed project.
  State result: set project.isSupported = true and mark gates stale; do not invent a new project.
  Receipt target: same factionId and projectId.

unsupport_project
  Request requires: factionId and projectId.
  State result: set project.isSupported = false and mark gates stale.
  Receipt target: same factionId and projectId.

retire_project
  Request requires: factionId and projectId of an existing completed project.
  State result: set project.status = retired, set project.isSupported = false, recompute factionStrength, mark gates stale.
  Receipt target: same factionId and projectId.

open_gates
  Request does not require a faction.
  State result: rebuild gates draft from current Shining factions, supported projects, residents, and radiance; write gates.hasOpenDraft = true and gates.isStale = false.
  Receipt target: empty ids, generatedDraftVersion = the resulting gates.draftVersion.
  Deterministic projection: copy `projectedStateFragment.afterFullShiningRoot.gates` from the client preview when present. If rebuilding manually, use radiance thresholds 0..99/100..219/220..379/380..579/580+, draft size 4/6/7/8/10, pick cap 1/2/2/3/4, supported remembrance projects for rerollsRemaining, source cards from faction heads + supported completed projects + resident descent sources, de-duplicate by dedupeKey, write all candidates to allCandidateBlessingCards, first draft-size cards to availableBlessingCards, their ids to shownBlessingCardIds, selectedBlessingCardIds=[], nextCandidateCursor=available count, and generatedDraftVersion=current gates.draftVersion+1.
```

Deterministic `open_gates` Projection summary:
- Prefer the client preview `projectedStateFragment.afterFullShiningRoot.gates` exactly.
- Manual rebuild uses radiance thresholds 0..99/100..219/220..379/380..579/580+, draft size 4/6/7/8/10, pick cap 1/2/2/3/4, deterministic candidate order, and `dedupeKey` de-duplication.
- The canonical output includes `allCandidateBlessingCards`, `availableBlessingCards`, `shownBlessingCardIds`, `selectedBlessingCardIds=[]`, `nextCandidateCursor`, `rerollsRemaining`, and `generatedDraftVersion`.

Every `coreActionReceipts[]` entry that closes `pending_shining_abode_actions.json` must echo the exact `quotedCostFeathers` and `quotedCostLightSparks` from the pending request, including zero-cost actions. Forge receipts have one extra audit rule: `forge_relic.retune_property` must echo the request `replacementProperty`, `forge_relic.uplift_rarity` must echo request `addedProperties`, and other forge actions must not invent those payload fields.

Correct `support_project` receipt fragment:

```json
  "coreActionReceipts": [
```

Detailed accepted fragments for the other faction/project actions:

The receipt alone is not enough. For these four actions, common invalid outputs are: receipt without the matching state mutation, mutation without the receipt, spending Light Sparks for support toggles, creating a new project for `support_project` or `unsupport_project`, deleting retired project history, or leaving an open gates draft fresh after faction/project inputs changed.

14B. Preparing the frozen next-life package:

`prepare_incarnation_package` is not the same turn as Shining pending-bootstrap handoff. This action freezes selected blessing cards into `preparedIncarnationPackage`. The later `TriggerIncarnation` handoff preserves that package for runtime consumption.

Accepted `prepare_incarnation_package` must:
- require a fresh open gates draft;
- use `sourceDraftVersion` matching current `gates.draftVersion`;
- use non-empty unique `selectedCardIds`;
- pending request must include `selectedCards[]` copied from canonical current `gates.availableBlessingCards[]` in the same order as `selectedCardIds[]`; matching only by id is not enough because `rarity`, `sourceType`, `effectFamily`, and `effectPayload` are part of the contract;
- write `preparedIncarnationPackage` with `selectedCardIds[]` and full `selectedCards[]`;
- reset/clean gates exactly as the canonical Shining helper does;
- not spend Ink Feathers by itself;
- include `selectedCards[]` in the receipt as the exact frozen package snapshot.

Correct receipt fragment:

```json
{
  "coreActionReceipts": [
    {
      "requestId": "shine_prepare_package_323",
      "actionType": "prepare_incarnation_package",
      "status": "accepted",
      "quotedCostFeathers": 0,
      "quotedCostLightSparks": 0,
      "factionId": "",
      "projectId": "",
      "relicId": "",
      "returnCycleId": "",
      "targetFormTag": "",
      "propertyIndex": -1,
      "selectedCardIds": [
        "card:shine_faction_glass_light_001:head:player_soul:route:rare"
      ],
      "selectedCards": [
        {
          "cardId": "card:shine_faction_glass_light_001:head:player_soul:route:rare",
          "dedupeKey": "route:{\"routeOptions\":1,\"latestTurn\":6}",
          "sourceType": "head",
          "sourceFactionId": "shine_faction_glass_light_001",
          "sourceActorId": "player_soul",
          "sourceFactionName": "Орден Стеклянного Света",
          "sourceActorName": "player_soul",
          "effectFamily": "route",
          "rarity": "rare",
          "displayName": "Дар фракции «Орден Стеклянного Света»",
          "displaySummary": "Откроет 1 ранний route option к 6 ходу.",
          "effectPayload": {
            "type": "seed_early_routes",
            "routeOptions": 1,
            "latestTurn": 6
          }
        }
      ],
      "newResidentIds": [],
      "seededProjectIds": [],
      "generatedDraftVersion": 7,
      "resolvedAtTurn": 323,
      "resolvedAtUtc": "2026-04-24T13:20:00Z",
      "reason": "selected blessing cards frozen for next incarnation"
    }
  ]
}
```

14C. Shining banner gacha:

`pull_relic_gacha` is a Shining faction-banner pull, not direct Chaos Sea `/gacha` and not Guardian-mediated `UpdateGuardians.processGacha`.

Accepted `pull_relic_gacha` must:
- use `turn_request.json.gachaBaseResult.baseRarity` as the minimum `baseRarity`;
- keep `finalRarity >= baseRarity`;
- never exceed `request.projectedGachaBonusSteps`;
- add exactly one new Soul Relic to `soul_state.json`;
- spend exact `quotedCostFeathers` and no Light Sparks unless the request says otherwise;
- update `shining_abode_state.json.gachaSystem.chargesUsedThisReturn`, `currentReturnCycleId`, and `gachaHistory[]`;
- write a receipt with `returnCycleId`, `relicId`, `relicName`, `baseRarity`, and `finalRarity`.

Correct receipt fragment:

```json
{
  "coreActionReceipts": [
    {
      "requestId": "shine_pull_glass_324",
      "actionType": "pull_relic_gacha",
      "status": "accepted",
      "quotedCostFeathers": 30,
      "quotedCostLightSparks": 0,
      "factionId": "shine_faction_glass_light_001",
      "projectId": "",
      "relicId": "relic_shining_glass_oath_324",
      "relicName": "Стеклянная Клятва Рассвета",
      "returnCycleId": "shining_return_9",
      "baseRarity": "Rare",
      "finalRarity": "Epic",
      "targetFormTag": "",
      "propertyIndex": -1,
      "selectedCardIds": [],
      "newResidentIds": [],
      "seededProjectIds": [],
      "generatedDraftVersion": 0,
      "resolvedAtTurn": 324,
      "resolvedAtUtc": "2026-04-24T13:30:00Z",
      "reason": "Shining faction banner pull resolved within projected bonus ceiling"
    }
  ]
}
```

14D. Shining forge actions:

All forge actions require ordinary active Shining Abode, a valid `factionId`, a real Soul Relic `relicId`, enough Ink Feathers and Light Sparks, and at least one supported completed `refinement` project in that faction. Accepted forge output must update both `shining_abode_state.json.lightSparks` and the exact Soul Relic in `soul_state.json`.

Exact Shining Forge Mutation Table / Forge action matrix:

```text
Global forge constants:
  Required Radiance tiers: reshape 0, retune_property 1, strengthen_band 2, stabilize_echo 3, uplift_rarity 4.
  Base costs: reshape 10 Feathers/10 Light Sparks; retune_property 20/15; strengthen_band 30/20; stabilize_echo 25/15; uplift_rarity 45/30.
  Service multiplier by factionStrength: 0..24 => 0.75, 25..49 => 1.0, 50..74 => 1.25, 75..100 => 1.5.
  Rarity/band ladder: common -> uncommon -> rare -> epic -> legendary; numeric bands increment by +1.
  Minimum property counts after rarity uplift: common 1, uncommon 2, rare 3, epic 4, legendary 5.
  Relic refinement entitlements consumed by forge must end as consumed(consumedAtTurn/consumedAtUtc), never remain pending after use. Do not write expired for relicRefinementEntitlements; expired(expiredAtTurn/expiredAtUtc) belongs only to supported route/lore/descent deferred effect arrays.

forge_relic.reshape
  Requires: relic.formTag exists, targetFormTag is non-empty and different.
  State result: relic.formTag becomes targetFormTag.
  Receipt identity: factionId, relicId, targetFormTag.

forge_relic.retune_property
  Requires: propertyIndex and replacementProperty with the same canonical band as the old property.
  State result: relic.properties[propertyIndex] becomes replacementProperty.
  Receipt identity: factionId, relicId, propertyIndex, exact replacementProperty copied from the request.

forge_relic.strengthen_band
  Requires: propertyIndex pointing to a property with upgradeable band.
  State result: relic.properties[propertyIndex].band upgrades by one canonical step.
  Receipt identity: factionId, relicId, propertyIndex.

forge_relic.stabilize_echo
  Requires: companion_echo relic or relic with companionSeed / companionManifestationQualityBonus.
  State result: relic.companionManifestationQualityBonus increases by floor(15 * service multiplier).
  Receipt identity: factionId, relicId.

forge_relic.uplift_rarity
  Requires: canonical rarity below legendary and enough addedProperties if the next rarity requires more properties.
  State result: relic rarity upgrades one step; missing minimum properties are appended from addedProperties.
  Receipt identity: factionId, relicId, exact addedProperties copied from the request.
```

Correct `forge_relic.retune_property` receipt fragment:

```json
{
  "coreActionReceipts": [
    {
      "requestId": "shine_forge_retune_325",
      "actionType": "forge_relic.retune_property",
      "status": "accepted",
      "quotedCostFeathers": 20,
      "quotedCostLightSparks": 15,
      "factionId": "shine_faction_glass_light_001",
      "projectId": "",
      "relicId": "relic_mirror_lens_001",
      "returnCycleId": "",
      "targetFormTag": "",
      "propertyIndex": 1,
      "replacementProperty": {
        "propertyId": "prop_mirror_lens_dawn_focus",
        "name": "Dawn Focus",
        "stat": "route_clarity",
        "band": "rare"
      },
      "selectedCardIds": [],
      "newResidentIds": [],
      "seededProjectIds": [],
      "generatedDraftVersion": 0,
      "resolvedAtTurn": 325,
      "resolvedAtUtc": "2026-04-24T13:40:00Z",
      "reason": "property at index 1 retuned within the same band"
    }
  ]
}
```

Canonical before/after fragment for that `retune_property` closure:

```json
{
  "changedSurfaces": [
    "game_state/meta/soul_state.json",
    "game_state/meta/shining_abode_state.json.coreActionReceipts[]",
    "game_state/meta/shining_abode_state.json.lightSparks"
  ],
  "beforeRelic": {

      {
        "propertyId": "prop_mirror_lens_old",

  "afterRelic": {
```

Other valid forge receipt fragments:

These are receipt fragments only. The same accepted response must also update `soul_state.json` with the full canonical Soul Relic result and update `shining_abode_state.json.lightSparks` by the exact quoted cost. Do not write a detached relic fragment that is not persisted in the Soul state.

Invalid Shining core-action shortcuts:
- Closing `pending_shining_abode_actions.json` only in prose.
- Writing `factionDataChanges` or `factionProjectUpdates` for Shining faction/project actions.
- Omitting empty receipt arrays such as `selectedCardIds`, `newResidentIds`, or `seededProjectIds`.
- Omitting exact receipt cost audit fields `quotedCostFeathers` and `quotedCostLightSparks`.
- Omitting the exact forge request payload from receipts (`replacementProperty` for retune, `addedProperties` for uplift).
- Accepted receipt with target ids that do not exactly match the pending request.
- `prepare_incarnation_package` receipt without `selectedCards[]` snapshot.
- `pull_relic_gacha` using `UpdateGuardians.processGacha` or direct `/gacha` rules.
- Forge action that changes a copied relic fragment instead of the canonical Soul Relic in `soul_state.json`.

==================================================
15. VALID — FREEFORM AFTERLIFE GUARDIAN COMMANDS
==================================================

The examples above focus on strict pending contracts. Ordinary afterlife roleplay with a Guardian may still use supported `UpdateGuardians` commands when the fiction justifies a concrete Guardian state change. Guardians are still not NPCs, and these commands are not Shining faction actions.

Supported non-create commands:

```text
updateReputation
completeQuest
processGacha
addMusings
unlockLore
setMood
```

Correct reasoning for a freeform Guardian scene:

```markdown
## Охват NPC-анализа
- Режим: Guardian-centric
- Релевантные акторы: Азалия
- Почему они релевантны: player asked Azalia for guidance and the response changes her reputation, mood, musing, and one lore fragment.
- Акторы вне охвата: other Guardians, residents, Shining factions
- Почему они вне охвата: the scene does not change their projects, relationships, resident state, or Shining institutions.

## Guardian Thoughts
### Азалия
- Ситуация: Душа признала прежнюю ошибку и попросила бережный способ помнить прошлую жизнь.
- Внутренние мысли: Азалия видит реальное изменение отношения, но не хочет превращать доверие в мгновенную награду.
- Действия: Она повышает доверие, фиксирует настроение, добавляет краткую мысль и открывает уже заслуженный lore fragment.
```

Correct `UpdateGuardians` fragment:

```json
{
  "UpdateGuardians": [
    {
      "command": "updateReputation",
      "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
      "reputationChange": 8,
      "reason": "The soul accepted responsibility and asked for memory guidance without demanding a reward."
    },
    {
      "command": "setMood",
      "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
      "mood": {
        "current": "contemplative",
        "intensity": 58,
        "reason": "Азалия увидела осторожное взросление души.",
        "since": 326
      }
    },
    {
      "command": "addMusings",
      "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
      "musings": [
        {
          "turn": 326,
          "topic": "soul_assessment",
          "mood": "hopeful",
          "text": "Душа впервые попросила память не как оружие, а как способ не повторить ту же боль."
        }
      ]
    },
    {
      "command": "unlockLore",
      "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
      "loreFragment": {
        "fragmentId": "lore_guard_social_azalia_001_domain",
        "category": "domain_mastery",
        "title": "Шелк памяти",
        "content": "Память Азалии не вытаскивает прошлое силой: она расправляет его слоями, пока душа сама не выдержит форму воспоминания.",
        "requiredReputation": 50
      }
    }
  ],
  "guardianThoughtJournalUpdates": [
    {
      "entryId": "guardian_thought_azalia_326_memory_guidance",
      "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
      "title": "Осторожная просьба",
      "summary": "Азалия решила, что душа заслужила мягкое раскрытие одного слоя её домена.",
      "turn": 326,
      "timestamp": "2026-04-24T13:50:00Z"
    }
  ]
}
```

Correct `completeQuest` fragment:

```json
{
  "UpdateGuardians": [
    {
      "command": "completeQuest",
      "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
      "questId": "gquest_azalia_memory_vow_002",
      "outcome": "success",
      "questPowerAudit": {
        "questDifficultyTier": "easy",
        "outcome": "success",
        "baseDelta": 2,
        "supportsCurrentProject": false,
        "defendsAgainstRivalPressure": false,
        "bonusDelta": 0,
        "finalDelta": 2
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

Rules for these commands:
- `updateReputation` uses top-level `reputationChange` and `reason`, not nested `data`.
- `completeQuest` can target only an existing active Guardian quest id in that Guardian's canonical `activeQuests[]`; it cannot close an unaccepted `availableQuests[]` offer.
- `completeQuest` always carries deterministic `questPowerAudit` with `questDifficultyTier`, `outcome`, `baseDelta`, `bonusDelta`, and `finalDelta`; do not write raw `guardianPowerEvents.reasonType = guardian_quest`.
- `processGacha` is only Guardian-mediated gacha; direct Chaos Sea `/gacha` must not use it.
- `addMusings`, `unlockLore`, and `setMood` use top-level `musings[]`, `loreFragment`, and `mood`.
- Every Guardian changed by `UpdateGuardians` must appear in `gm_thoughts_markdown` relevant actors with a matching reasoning block.

Invalid Guardian command shortcuts:
- `UpdateNPCs` for Guardian mood, speech, or reputation.
- `UpdateGuardians.updateProject`; use `startGuardianProjects`, `guardianProjectUpdates`, and `completeGuardianProjects`.
- `processGacha` when the Guardian has no remaining charge this return.
- `unlockLore` with arbitrary text instead of a full `loreFragment` object.
- `setMood` without `current`, `intensity`, `reason`, and `since`.

==================================================
16. VALID — COMBINED CHAOS SEA SCHEDULER + OFFERING + PLAYER ACTION
==================================================

Scenario:
- Realm is ordinary `Chaos Sea`.
- The player action is an Ink Feather Abode offering.
- `progressionControl` also requires Chaos Sea hub, Guardian project, and resident agency cycles.
- There is no Shining state involved in this turn.

Input summary:

```json
{
  "turn_request.json": {
    "sessionId": "afterlife_session_combo_401",
    "requestId": "afterlife_turn_combo_401",
    "turnNumber": 401,
    "playerAction": "[INK_FEATHER_ACTION: ABODE_OFFERING] Я отдаю 100 Чернильных Перьев Обители Азалии.",
    "progressionControl": {
      "chaosSeaCyclesExpectedThisTurn": 1,
      "guardianProjectCyclesExpectedThisTurn": 1,
      "residentAgencyCyclesExpectedThisTurn": 1,
      "nextChaosSeaTurnOrdinal": 44,
      "nextGuardianProjectCycleOrdinal": 25,
      "nextResidentAgencyCycleOrdinal": 19,
      "mustEvaluateChaosSeaProgression": true,
      "mustEvaluateGuardianProjectProgression": true,
      "mustEvaluateResidentAgencyProgression": true,
      "afterlifeCatchupRequired": false
    }
  },
  "pending_abode_offering.json": {
    "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
    "guardianName": "Азалия",
    "abodeId": "abode_azalia_memory_silk_001",
    "offeringType": "ink_feathers",
    "inkFeathersOffered": 100,
    "returnCycleId": "return_8",
    "createdAtUtc": "2026-04-24T15:00:00Z"
  }
}
```

Correct GM reasoning:

```markdown
## Охват NPC-анализа
- Режим: Mixed
- Afterlife subtype: Chaos Sea afterlife combined turn.
- Релевантные акторы: Азалия, Лиора
- Почему они релевантны: Азалия получает offering и её проект получает due cycle; Лиора получает resident agency cycle.
- Акторы вне охвата: Элиан, другие резиденты, Shining factions
- Почему они вне охвата: этот ход не меняет их projects, receipts, faction state or resident memory.

## Guardian Thoughts
### Азалия
- Ситуация: Душа приносит offering, а scheduler одновременно требует один проектный цикл.
- Мысли: Азалия принимает offering как укрепление Обители, но не превращает его в mortal-world награду.
- Действия: Азалия усиливает Обитель через guardianPowerEvents и продвигает текущий проект через guardianProjectUpdates.

## Resident Thoughts
### Лиора
- Ситуация: В Обители стало спокойнее после offering, и у Лиоры есть due resident agency.
- Мысли: Лиора решает зафиксировать новую осторожную надежду, но не раскрывает историю без pending history request.
- Действия: Лиора получает thought journal entry; resident cycle считается обработанным без talk/history receipt.

## Afterlife Living World / Живой мир Посмертия
- Chaos Sea hub cycle: течение душ вокруг Обители стабилизируется после offering; это отражено в response, не в worldEventsLog.
- Guardian project cycle: один цикл Азалии продвинут в project tracker.
- Resident agency cycle: Лиора действует через residentThoughtJournalUpdates.
- No Shining contours are due.
```

Correct response/state fragment:

```json
{
  "response": "Море Хаоса не считает подношение отдельной сценой от жизни Обители. Чернильные Перья растворяются в шёлковых стенах Азалии, и вокруг её зала стихает давний шум чужих воспоминаний. Лиора впервые смотрит на этот покой не как на клетку, а как на возможность дождаться следующего честного разговора.",
  "gm_thoughts_markdown": "## Охват NPC-анализа\n- Режим: Mixed\n- Afterlife subtype: Chaos Sea afterlife combined turn.\n- Релевантные акторы: Азалия, Лиора\n- Почему они релевантны: Азалия получает offering и её проект получает due cycle; Лиора получает resident agency cycle.\n- Акторы вне охвата: Элиан, другие резиденты, Shining factions\n- Почему они вне охвата: этот ход не меняет их projects, receipts, faction state or resident memory.\n\n## Guardian Thoughts\n### Азалия\n- Ситуация: Душа приносит offering, а scheduler одновременно требует один проектный цикл.\n- Мысли: Азалия принимает offering как укрепление Обители, но не превращает его в mortal-world награду.\n- Действия: Азалия усиливает Обитель через guardianPowerEvents и продвигает текущий проект через guardianProjectUpdates.\n\n## Resident Thoughts\n### Лиора\n- Ситуация: В Обители стало спокойнее после offering, и у Лиоры есть due resident agency.\n- Мысли: Лиора решает зафиксировать новую осторожную надежду, но не раскрывает историю без pending history request.\n- Действия: Лиора получает thought journal entry; resident cycle считается обработанным без talk/history receipt.\n\n## Afterlife Living World / Живой мир Посмертия\n- Chaos Sea hub cycle: течение душ вокруг Обители стабилизируется после offering; это отражено в response, не в worldEventsLog.\n- Guardian project cycle: один цикл Азалии продвинут в project tracker.\n- Resident agency cycle: Лиора действует через residentThoughtJournalUpdates.\n- No Shining contours are due.",
  "guardianProjectUpdates": [
```

==================================================
17. VALID — COMBINED ACTIVE SHINING SCHEDULER + CORE ACTION + TRADE
==================================================

Scenario:
- Realm is ordinary active `Shining Abode`.
- `preparedIncarnationPackage` is null, so this is not a bootstrap handoff.
- The turn has Shining Abode, Shining faction, Shining trade, Guardian project, and resident agency debt.
- `pending_shining_abode_actions.json` asks to support a completed project.
- Pre-turn `shining_abode_state.gates.hasOpenDraft` is true; supporting the project changes gate inputs, so the accepted state must preserve the draft and set `gates.isStale=true`.
- `pending_shining_trade_inventory_requests.json` asks to materialize a Shining faction trade inventory.

Correct GM reasoning:

```markdown
## Охват NPC-анализа
- Режим: Mixed
- Afterlife subtype: Active Shining Abode combined turn.
- Релевантные акторы: Орден Стеклянного Света, Элиан, Мира
- Почему они релевантны: Орден получает support_project и trade inventory; Элиан получает guardian project cycle; Мира получает resident agency cycle.
- Акторы вне охвата: другие Shining factions, inactive halls, Chaos Sea residents
- Почему они вне охвата: их contracts absent, and no due contour requires their direct state mutation.

## Shining Institution Thoughts
### Орден Стеклянного Света
- Ситуация: Душа публично поддержала completed project and requested a trade display.
- Мысли: Институция связывает поддержку проекта с публичной дисциплиной торговли, not with Mortal World factionDataChanges.
- Действия: Орден marks the project supported, prepares exact four-slot trade inventory, and writes matching core/trade receipts.

## Guardian Thoughts
### Элиан
- Ситуация: Shining civic order now supports his gate-stability project.
- Мысли: Элиан treats the institutional support as a meaningful assist, not a separate NPC scene.
- Действия: Элиан advances his guardian project through guardianProjectUpdates.

## Resident Thoughts
### Мира
- Ситуация: Public support makes the next passage less frightening for residents.
- Мысли: Мира chooses to help visitors read the glass rules before they enter.
- Действия: Мира receives residentThoughtJournalUpdates; no transfer or roster receipt is invented.

## Afterlife Living World / Живой мир Посмертия
- Shining Abode cycle: civic order stabilizes around the supported project.
- Shining faction cycle: the faction's project support becomes canonical Shining state.
- Shining trade cycle: exact pending trade inventory is materialized.
- Gates side effect: because `support_project` changes inputs for the open gates draft, preserve `gates` but set `gates.isStale=true`; do not prepare an incarnation package from this stale draft.
- Guardian project and resident agency cycles are processed through their own afterlife surfaces.
```

==================================================
18. VALID — PREPARE PACKAGE NOW, BOOTSTRAP LATER
==================================================

This is the most important Shining lifecycle distinction:

```text
prepare_incarnation_package turn:
- Realm: ordinary active Shining Abode.
- preparedIncarnationPackage is currently null.
- `pending_shining_abode_actions.json` root is `{ "requests": [ ... ] }`; the active request has `actionType = prepare_incarnation_package`.
- GM closes the core action through canonical Shining state mutation and coreActionReceipts[].
- GM writes/updates preparedIncarnationPackage with selectedCardIds[] and selectedCards[].
- GM does NOT write TriggerIncarnation on this same accepted turn, even if the player asks to incarnate immediately.
- A separate explicit incarnation request is handled only on a later pending-bootstrap handoff turn, after preparedIncarnationPackage already exists in the pre-turn state.

later pending-bootstrap handoff turn:
- Realm: Shining Abode.
- preparedIncarnationPackage already exists before the turn starts.
- This is not ordinary active Shining roleplay.
- GM writes TriggerIncarnation only.
- GM preserves preparedIncarnationPackage exactly as provided.
- Runtime consumes and clears the package after successful Mortal World bootstrap.
```

Correct active Shining `prepare_incarnation_package` closure shape:

```json
{
```
```json
  "response": "Сияющая Обитель удерживает уже выбранный узор и не требует новой политики. Душа проходит через подготовленные врата, а frozen package остаётся на месте до runtime consumption.",
  "gm_thoughts_markdown": "## Охват NPC-анализа\n- Режим: Scene-local\n- Afterlife subtype: Shining pending-bootstrap handoff.\n- Релевантные акторы: нет\n- Почему они релевантны: обычные actor systems suppressed; the handoff only writes TriggerIncarnation.\n- Акторы вне охвата: Guardians, residents, Shining factions\n- Почему они вне охвата: preparedIncarnationPackage already exists and ordinary Shining progression is suppressed.\n\n## Afterlife Living World / Живой мир Посмертия\n- No ordinary Shining scheduler cycles are processed in this handoff.\n- preparedIncarnationPackage is preserved exactly for runtime bootstrap consumption.\n- Accepted-turn authority: TriggerIncarnation is valid only with the matching validated game_state/control/pending_turn_snapshot context for this accepted turn.",
  "TriggerIncarnation": {
    "worldDescription": "Мир речных храмов, где архивы спорят с духами наводнений.",
    "characterDescription": "Ребёнок хранителя храмовой библиотеки, родившийся рядом с затопленным порогом.",
    "circumstances": "Семья должна выбрать, какие записи спасти до первого большого паводка.",
    "source": "shining_prepared_package"
  }
}
```

Do not write during the later handoff:

```text
preparedIncarnationPackage = null
preparedIncarnationPackageId on TriggerIncarnation
ordinary Shining coreActionReceipts[]
ordinary Shining trade/faction/resident progression report
soul_state.currentRealm = "Mortal World"
manual/out-of-band incarnation_trigger.json without validated accepted-turn authority
```

==================================================
19. VALID — ORDINARY CHAOS SEA LIVING WORLD WITHOUT PENDING FILES
==================================================

This example is for an ordinary `Chaos Sea` turn where no pending request file exists, but the scheduler still says the world is alive. The GM must not answer with dialogue only. Due contours still need reasoning, concrete afterlife consequences or a stability explanation, and a progression report.

Input conditions:

```json
{
  "turn_request.json": {
    "sessionId": "session_afterlife_019",
    "requestId": "turn_419",
    "turnNumber": 419,
    "currentRealm": "Chaos Sea",
    "playerAction": "Я молча сижу у края Обители Азалии и слушаю Море.",
    "progressionControl": {
      "currentRealm": "Chaos Sea",
      "currentChaosSeaTurnOrdinal": 37,
      "nextChaosSeaTurnOrdinal": 38,
      "lastChaosSeaSimulationOrdinal": 36,
      "lastGuardianProjectCycleOrdinal": 21,
      "nextGuardianProjectCycleOrdinal": 38,
      "lastResidentAgencyCycleOrdinal": 14,
      "nextResidentAgencyCycleOrdinal": 38,
      "chaosSeaCyclesExpectedThisTurn": 1,
      "guardianProjectCyclesExpectedThisTurn": 1,
      "residentAgencyCyclesExpectedThisTurn": 1,
      "mustEvaluateChaosSeaProgression": true,
      "mustEvaluateGuardianProjectProgression": true,
      "mustEvaluateResidentAgencyProgression": true,
      "afterlifeCatchupRequired": false,
      "afterlifeCatchupSummaryEventsRequired": 0,
      "afterlifeCatchupContours": []
    }
  }
}
```

Correct reasoning:

```markdown
## Охват NPC-анализа
- Режим: Guardian-centric
- Afterlife subtype: Ordinary Chaos Sea living-world turn without pending files.
- Релевантные акторы: Азалия, Лиора
- Почему они релевантны: Азалия has a due Guardian project cycle; Лиора has a due resident agency cycle.
- Акторы вне охвата: other Guardians, Shining factions
- Почему они вне охвата: no Shining realm state is active, no pending Shining files exist, and the hub cycle produces local Abode pressure only.

## Guardian Thoughts
### Азалия
- Ситуация: The player is silent, but the Sea's pressure around her Abode still changes.
- Мысли: Азалия notices that silence gives her current project a clean anchor instead of a new demand.
- Действия: She advances the existing project by one small work step and records a thought.

## Resident Thoughts
### Лиора
- Ситуация: The quieter Abode gives Лиора enough safety to act internally.
- Мысли: She decides not to request a conversation yet, but her agency cycle still matters.
- Действия: She records a resident thought; no roster, transfer, or history request is invented.

## Afterlife Living World / Живой мир Посмертия
- Chaos Sea hub cycle: the Sea calms around this Abode rather than creating a new external event; this is described in response and closed by progression report.
- Guardian project cycle: Азалия advances her existing project through `guardianProjectUpdates`.
- Resident agency cycle: Лиора acts through `residentThoughtJournalUpdates`.
- No pending files exist, so no receipt arrays are invented.
```

Correct accepted response fragment:

```json

  "response": "Море Хаоса не отвечает словами. Оно чуть отступает от края Обители, оставляя Азалии редкую неподвижность для работы, а Лиоре — место, где память не требует немедленного разговора.",
```

Why this is valid:
- There are no pending files, so no closure receipts are invented.
- The player action is quiet, but scheduler debt still produces living-world consequences.
- The Chaos Sea hub cycle is not written to `worldEventsLog`.
- Every processed contour advances only its own marker.
- Resident agency is not skipped just because the resident did not speak to the player.

==================================================
20. VALID — SYSTEM ETERNAL GUARDIAN ATTRACTION
==================================================

This example is for a deterministic Eternal Guardian attraction from ordinary Chaos Sea. The player-facing fiction is "I seek this Eternal Guardian." The technical contract is `game_state/control/system_guardian_attraction.json`, often paired with `guardians.json.pendingGuardianCreation.mode = system_preset`; do not create or resolve it from Shining Abode.

The GM must not improvise a nearby Guardian archetype. The result must point to the requested preset and persist `sourcePreset` on the materialized Guardian.

Important runtime marker: the canonical `turn_request.json.playerAction` for this flow starts with `[CHAOS_SEA_SYSTEM_GUARDIAN_ATTRACTION: <presetId>]`. This is a client-authored routing tag. Keep it in the input/audit context so the runtime can clear `system_guardian_attraction.json` after acceptance, but do not present the tag to the player as diegetic speech.

Input fragments:

```json
{
  "turn_request.json": {
    "sessionId": "system_attraction_session_020",
    "requestId": "turn_420",
    "turnNumber": 420,
    "currentRealm": "Chaos Sea",
    "playerAction": "[CHAOS_SEA_SYSTEM_GUARDIAN_ATTRACTION: azalia] Я ищу Вечную Хранительницу Азалию.",
    "progressionControl": {
      "currentRealm": "Chaos Sea",
      "mustEvaluateChaosSeaProgression": false,
      "mustEvaluateGuardianProjectProgression": false,
      "mustEvaluateResidentAgencyProgression": false,
      "afterlifeCatchupRequired": false
    }
  },
  "system_guardian_attraction.json": {
    "mode": "system_guardian_attraction",
    "targetPresetId": "azalia",
    "targetPresetDisplayName": "Азалия",
    "targetPresetVersion": "1.0",
    "sourceLibrary": "built_in",
    "targetSummary": "Дипломатичная хранительница страсти, власти и преданности.",
    "renderedPromptPackage": "PresetId: azalia"
  },
  "guardians.json": {
    "guardians": [],
    "activeGuardian": null,
    "pendingGuardianCreation": {
      "mode": "system_preset",
      "presetId": "azalia",
      "presetDisplayName": "Азалия",
      "presetVersion": "1.0",
      "sourceLibrary": "built_in"
    }
  }
}
```

Correct reasoning:

```markdown
## Охват NPC-анализа
- Режим: Guardian-centric
- Afterlife subtype: System Eternal Guardian attraction.
- Релевантные акторы: Азалия
- Почему они релевантны: `system_guardian_attraction.json.targetPresetId = azalia`, so this turn must materialize or route to that exact Eternal Guardian.
- Акторы вне охвата: invented Guardians, Mortal World NPCs, Shining factions
- Почему они вне охвата: the attraction is deterministic and has no Mortal World or Shining faction contract.

## Guardian Thoughts
### Азалия
- Ситуация: Душа ищет именно Азалию as a built-in Eternal Guardian preset.
- Мысли: Азалия recognizes the deterministic call and answers through her own silk-memory domain, not through a substitute archetype.
- Действия: She manifests with stable canonical identity, discovered abode, and sourcePreset metadata.

## Contract audit
- `sourcePreset.presetId` remains `azalia`.
- `playerAction` contains `[CHAOS_SEA_SYSTEM_GUARDIAN_ATTRACTION: azalia]`, so runtime may clear the attraction control file after accepted processing.
- `activeGuardian.guardianId` points to the materialized Azalia Guardian.
- `chaosSeaNavigation.currentAbodeId` points to Azalia's discovered Abode.
- No `UpdateNPCs` or Mortal World state is written.
```

==================================================
21. VALID — PROTECTED FIRST AFTERLIFE TURN AFTER MORTAL LIFE
==================================================

This example is for the first ordinary afterlife turn after Life Evaluation returned the soul to the Chaos Sea. `afterlife_return_guard.json` is a client-owned safety guard. The GM reads it, respects it, and leaves it alone.

Input fragments:

```json
{
  "turn_request.json": {
    "sessionId": "protected_return_session_021",
    "requestId": "turn_421",
    "turnNumber": 421,
    "currentRealm": "Chaos Sea",
    "playerAction": "Я просто возвращаюсь в Обитель и пытаюсь понять, что произошло.",
    "progressionControl": {
      "currentRealm": "Chaos Sea",
      "mustEvaluateChaosSeaProgression": false,
      "mustEvaluateGuardianProjectProgression": false,
      "mustEvaluateResidentAgencyProgression": false,
      "afterlifeCatchupRequired": false
    }
  },
  "afterlife_return_guard.json": {
    "reason": "post_life_return",
    "remainingProtectedTurns": 1,
    "activatedAtUtc": "2026-04-24T16:20:00Z",
    "activatedAtTurnNumber": 420,
    "guardianId": "guard_social_azalia_001",
    "guardianName": "Азалия"
  }
}
```

Correct reasoning:

```markdown
## Охват NPC-анализа
- Режим: Guardian-centric
- Afterlife subtype: Protected first afterlife turn after mortal life.
- Релевантные акторы: Азалия, Лиора
- Почему они релевантны: `afterlife_return_guard.json` names Азалия as current guardian context, and the player is returning to her Abode; Лиора is present in the current Abode roster and must be deliberately kept stable rather than silently rewritten.
- Акторы вне охвата: forced reincarnation route, Shining re-entry, Mortal World NPCs
- Почему они вне охвата: the guard requires one ordinary afterlife turn before any Guardian-forced reincarnation and is not a Shining bootstrap marker.

## Guardian Thoughts
### Азалия
- Ситуация: Душа только что вернулась из смертной жизни.
- Мысли: Азалия notices exhaustion and refuses to punish or immediately throw the soul into another body.
- Действия: She provides a quiet ordinary afterlife scene and does not create an incarnation trigger.

## Resident Thoughts
### Лиора
- Ситуация: Душа вернулась в Обитель, где Лиора уже присутствует as a resident.
- Мысли: Лиора recognizes that this is not her request or history-reveal turn.
- Действия: She remains stable in the roster; no resident receipt, transfer, history reveal, or companion manifestation is invented.

## Lifecycle guard audit
- `afterlife_return_guard.json` is preserved exactly; the GM does not clear, decrement, or rewrite it.
- No `TriggerIncarnation` is written, especially no `source=guardian_forced`.
- No Shining bootstrap or re-entry is invented.
- No Mortal World state is written.
- If the guard is malformed instead of valid, treat that as fail-closed and do not weaken protection.
```

Correct accepted response fragment:

```json
{
  "response": "Море Хаоса не толкает тебя обратно в новую жизнь. Азалия встречает тебя у входа в Обитель и на этот раз не требует решения. Она просто оставляет рядом тёплую серебряную ткань, чтобы память о смерти стала тише.",
  "gm_thoughts_markdown": "## Охват NPC-анализа\n- Режим: Guardian-centric\n- Afterlife subtype: Protected first afterlife turn after mortal life.\n- Релевантные акторы: Азалия, Лиора\n- Почему они релевантны: `afterlife_return_guard.json` names Азалия as current guardian context, and the player is returning to her Abode; Лиора is present in the current Abode roster and must be deliberately kept stable rather than silently rewritten.\n- Акторы вне охвата: forced reincarnation route, Shining re-entry, Mortal World NPCs\n- Почему они вне охвата: the guard requires one ordinary afterlife turn before any Guardian-forced reincarnation and is not a Shining bootstrap marker.\n\n## Guardian Thoughts\n### Азалия\n- Ситуация: Душа только что вернулась из смертной жизни.\n- Мысли: Азалия notices exhaustion and refuses to punish or immediately throw the soul into another body.\n- Действия: She provides a quiet ordinary afterlife scene and does not create an incarnation trigger.\n\n## Resident Thoughts\n### Лиора\n- Ситуация: Душа вернулась в Обитель, где Лиора уже присутствует as a resident.\n- Мысли: Лиора recognizes that this is not her request or history-reveal turn.\n- Действия: She remains stable in the roster; no resident receipt, transfer, history reveal, or companion manifestation is invented.\n\n## Lifecycle guard audit\n- `afterlife_return_guard.json` is preserved exactly; the GM does not clear, decrement, or rewrite it.\n- No `TriggerIncarnation` is written, especially no `source=guardian_forced`.\n- No Shining bootstrap or re-entry is invented.\n- No Mortal World state is written.\n- If the guard is malformed instead of valid, treat that as fail-closed and do not weaken protection."
}
```

Why this is valid:
- The active guard blocks only immediate Guardian-forced reincarnation; it does not block ordinary afterlife narration.
- The GM does not mutate the client-owned guard. Runtime consumes/clears it after accepting the protected ordinary afterlife turn.
- That consumption rule applies only to a semantic-valid guard with `reason = post_life_return`. If `afterlife_return_guard.json` is malformed, unreadable, or parsed with the wrong `reason`, the GM leaves it intact; the route remains fail-closed until validation repair or explicit client/runtime clear.
- For `return_to_chaos_sea`, Shining pending files with non-empty `requests[]` or malformed JSON block the local route. A valid explicit-empty `{ "requests": [] }` file is not an actionable GM contract and may be removed by runtime as stale local state. `shining_abode_state.json.pendingNativeFactionDiscovery` must be exactly `null`; any non-null object, string, array, or malformed legacy evidence blocks return until repaired or closed.
- The response does not confuse `afterlife_return_guard.json` with `preparedIncarnationPackage`, `AscensionTrigger`, or Mortal World return state.

==================================================
22. VALID — DIRECT RESIDENT ACTION TAGS AND PENDING-BACKED ROUTING TAGS
==================================================

This example is a routing reference for exact tags that may appear inside `input/turn_request.json.playerAction`. The GM must recognize the tag first, then choose the matching row in `OtherGuides/Afterlife_Contract_Matrix.md`.

These tags are not all the same kind of contract:
- `[ABODE_RESIDENT_RELIC_GRANT]` and `[ABODE_RESIDENT_QUEST_REQUEST]` are direct resident actions. They have no pending control file and close no receipt arrays by themselves.
- `[GUARDIAN_TRADE_REQUEST]`, `[GUARDIAN_SOCIAL_TALK_REQUEST]`, `[GUARDIAN_SOCIAL_LORE_REQUEST]`, `[ARCHIVE_CONSULTATION_REQUEST]`, `[ARCHIVE_PROJECT_FUEL_REQUEST]`, `[ABODE_RESIDENT_ROSTER_REQUEST]`, `[ABODE_RESIDENT_HISTORY_REQUEST]`, `[ABODE_RESIDENT_TALK]`, `[ABODE_RESIDENT_TRANSFER_REQUEST]`, and `[PLAYER_GUARDIAN_FOUNDATION]` are pending-backed routing markers. The GM must read the matching pending file and close that exact pending contract.

Direct resident reward:

```text
playerAction:
[ABODE_RESIDENT_RELIC_GRANT] Игрок принимает реликвию связи от afterlife resident 'Лиора' (residentId=res_azalia_liora_001, guardianId=guard_social_azalia_001, abodeId=abode_azalia_memory_silk_001).

Required accepted-turn surfaces:
- metaStateUpdates.soulRelicOperations.addRelic:
  - this must be a new relic added by this accepted turn; an already stored companion echo is not enough
  - relicType = companion_echo
  - sourceResidentId = res_azalia_liora_001
  - sourceGuardianId = guard_social_azalia_001
  - companionSeed is complete: companionNameHint, originWorldSummary, futureCompanionPrompt, bondReason, coreTraits, archetypeHints, appearanceMotifs, personalityProfile, abodeDisposition, abodeDevotionLevel / abodeDevotionTier, restlessness / migrationState
- UpdateGuardianAbodeResidents:
  - same residentId / guardianId / abodeId
  - this must transition the existing pre-turn resident in this accepted turn
  - bondRewardState = granted
  - grantedRelicId = the new relic id
  - optional bounded devotion/restlessness changes when the scene justifies them
- residentInteractionLogUpdates:
  - new current-turn concise memory of why the resident granted the relic

Do not satisfy this tag with stale state:
- a pre-existing `companion_echo` relic with the same `sourceResidentId` is not a new reward
- a resident that was already granted (`bondRewardState=granted`) to the same `grantedRelicId` before the turn is not a current-turn reward transition
- an old interaction log entry is not enough; write a new `residentInteractionLogUpdates` entry for this accepted turn

Forbidden for this tag:
- no UpdateGuardianAbodeResidentInteractionReceipts
- no UpdateGuardianAbodeResidentRosterReceipts
- no UpdateGuardianAbodeResidentTransferReceipts
- no pending_resident_companion_manifestation_request.json output
- no UpdateNPCs
```

Direct resident quest:

```text
playerAction:
[ABODE_RESIDENT_QUEST_REQUEST] Игрок помогает обитателю загробья 'Лиора' (residentId=res_azalia_liora_001, guardianId=guard_social_azalia_001, abodeId=abode_azalia_memory_silk_001).

Required accepted-turn surfaces:
- UpdateSoulQuests:
  - this must create a new Soul Quest or progress/change an existing resident-linked Soul Quest in this accepted turn
  - ordinary Soul Quest identity, title, description, status, and objective data
  - guardianId = guard_social_azalia_001 when the quest belongs to that Abode
  - relatedAfterlifeResidentId = res_azalia_liora_001
- UpdateGuardianAbodeResidents when resident state changes:
  - linkedSoulQuestId = the new or progressed Soul Quest id
  - optional bondLevel / bondTier / devotion changes when justified
- residentInteractionLogUpdates:
  - new current-turn concise memory that the resident asked for or accepted this quest path

Do not satisfy this tag with stale state:
- an unchanged pre-existing Soul Quest with `relatedAfterlifeResidentId` is not enough
- an old resident interaction log entry is not enough; write a new `residentInteractionLogUpdates` entry for this accepted turn
- use `UpdateSoulQuests` / `soul_quests.json`; do not route through Mortal `UpdateQuests`

Forbidden for this tag:
- no Mortal UpdateQuests
- no UpdateNPCs
- no resident receipt arrays unless a separate pending resident file exists in game_state/control
```
