Pending-backed routing tags:

```text
[GUARDIAN_TRADE_REQUEST]
Read game_state/control/pending_guardian_trade_request.json.
Close with guardians[].tradeInventory plus UpdateGuardianTradeInventoryReceipts.
Do not place commandless tradeInventory inside UpdateGuardians.

[GUARDIAN_SOCIAL_TALK_REQUEST]
Read game_state/control/pending_guardian_social_interactions.json.
Close the matching interactionType=talk request through guardianSocialJournalUpdates.
Allowed Guardian responseMode values: talk_scene, lore_revealed, lore_refused, warning, refusal, trust_shift, attitude_shift.

[GUARDIAN_SOCIAL_LORE_REQUEST]
Read game_state/control/pending_guardian_social_interactions.json.
Close the matching interactionType=lore request through guardianSocialJournalUpdates.
For a lore request use lore_revealed when lore is actually revealed; use lore_refused, warning, or refusal when the Guardian withholds it.

[ARCHIVE_CONSULTATION_REQUEST]
Read game_state/control/pending_archive_consultation_request.json.
Close with archiveActionResolutions and soul_state.afterlifeArchive.actionReceipts[].
Do not create Mortal World lore, inventory, or notification output as the closure.

[ARCHIVE_PROJECT_FUEL_REQUEST]
Read game_state/control/pending_archive_project_fuel_request.json.
Close with archiveActionResolutions.
lore_fragment may resolve as project_work; secret_record resolves only as pressure_relief.

[ABODE_RESIDENT_ROSTER_REQUEST]
Read game_state/control/pending_guardian_abode_residents_request.json.
Close with full UpdateGuardianAbodeResidents objects plus UpdateGuardianAbodeResidentRosterReceipts.
Do not emit identity-only residents.

[ABODE_RESIDENT_HISTORY_REQUEST]
Read game_state/control/pending_guardian_abode_resident_interactions.json.
Close the matching interactionType=history request with UpdateGuardianAbodeResidentInteractionReceipts.
Accepted history requires UpdateGuardianAbodeResidentHistoryLog and a receipt historyEntryId that points to that entry.
Allowed resident responseMode values: talk_scene, history_revealed, history_refused, history_partial, bond_shift_only.

[ABODE_RESIDENT_TALK]
Read game_state/control/pending_guardian_abode_resident_interactions.json.
Close the matching interactionType=talk request with UpdateGuardianAbodeResidentInteractionReceipts.
Use talk_scene for a normal conversation, bond_shift_only when only bond/devotion changes, and do not invent a history log for plain talk.

[ABODE_RESIDENT_TRANSFER_REQUEST]
Read game_state/control/pending_guardian_abode_resident_transfers.json.
Close with UpdateGuardianAbodeResidentTransferReceipts, matching resident state changes, and departure/arrival history.
Preserve selectionMode and competition metadata from the pending request:
- selectionMode = competition_recommended | manual_override | departure_only
- competitionScore = integer 0..100 when present
- competitionLabel uses only `strong_pull`, `plausible_pull`, or `weak_pull`; competitionReason explains why the target Abode won or why the player overrode it
- departure_only must not carry target Guardian/Abode competition metadata

[PLAYER_GUARDIAN_FOUNDATION]
Read game_state/control/pending_player_guardian_foundation.json.
Close with UpdateGuardians.create, canonical guardians, activeGuardian, chaosSeaNavigation, soul foundation fields, and playerGuardianFoundationHistory.
Do not change patron roles without the authorized foundation create.
```

Client-local Soul Relic equip/unequip preview:

```text
This preview is not an input/turn_request.json playerAction and not a GM contract.

The player may see a local preview such as:
- Экипировать Реликвию Души: move one relic from soulRelics.stored[] to soulRelics.equipped[].
- Снять Реликвию Души: move one relic from soulRelics.equipped[] to soulRelics.stored[].

Required handling:
- no GM turn is sent
- no pending/control file is created
- no receipt array is written
- no progression_report.json is written
- no gm_thoughts_markdown is required
- no existing pending contract is closed
- only the client applies the listed client-local mutation to game_state/meta/soul_state.json after player confirmation

The GM must not invent an accepted-turn response for this preview and must not add UpdateGuardians, UpdateNPCs, output files, afterlife notifications, or Mortal World state for it.
```

==================================================
23. VALID — FREEFORM CHAOS SEA ABODE SEARCH
==================================================

This example covers the free search mode inside `/обители` -> `Искать новую обитель` -> `Свободный поиск мыслью`.

The client shows the player a preview, but does not create a pending/control file. The next typed free-text player action is an ordinary GM-authored Chaos Sea turn. The GM must resolve the search through afterlife Guardian/Abode surfaces only.

Input pattern:

```text
currentRealm: Chaos Sea
playerAction:
Ищу хранителя боевых искусств, который сможет научить душу дисциплине без жестокости.
```

Mandatory contract:
- This is not client-local. The GM writes an accepted afterlife turn if the search has narrative or state consequences.
- There is no `game_state/control/pending_*` file for freeform Abode search.
- The GM must explicitly show the d20-style outcome tier from the prompt fiction: failure, distant signal, found Abode, or rare Guardian.
- The GM must not use Mortal World travel/location systems.
- Any stateful outcome must carry explicit ids and source reasoning: `guardianId`, `guardianName`, `abodeId` or location label, and `reason/source = chaos_sea_abode_search`.

Outcome A — failure:

```text
Allowed:
- response describes the failed search and why the Sea gives no stable route.
- gm_thoughts_markdown explains that no Guardian/Abode was materialized.

Forbidden:
- no hidden Guardian in guardians.json
- no activeGuardian change
- no chaosSeaNavigation.currentAbodeId change
- no pending file invented for a future search
- no currentLocationData, UpdateNPCs, worldEventsLog, timeChange, or weatherChange
```

Outcome B — distant signal:

```text
Allowed:
- response describes a distant clue, omen, name-fragment, domain trace, or silhouette.
- if the clue is only narrative, do not write state.
- if the clue becomes stateful, materialize an explicit Guardian/Abode clue through supported Guardian surfaces and include guardianId, guardianName, abodeId/location label, and reason/source = chaos_sea_abode_search.

Forbidden:
- do not teleport the player silently.
- do not set activeGuardian/currentAbodeId unless the fiction says the soul actually arrives.
- do not create an anonymous pending/nearby clue file outside the documented control-file matrix.
```

Outcome C — found Abode / rare Guardian:

```text
Required accepted-turn surfaces:
- UpdateGuardians.create when the Guardian is new:
  - command = create
  - data = full canonical Guardian object, not an identity-only fragment
  - data.guardianId is stable and unique
  - data.canonicalName / manifestation display name matches the found Guardian
  - data.abode.abodeId is stable and unique
  - data.abode.name or location label is non-empty
  - data.abode.isDiscovered = true
  - relationship/mood/reputation context records that this first contact came from reason/source = chaos_sea_abode_search
- If the Guardian already exists, use only supported Guardian update surfaces and preserve the canonical object.
- Canonical guardians.json roots:
  - activeGuardian.guardianId = found guardianId when the player arrives there now
  - chaosSeaNavigation.currentAbodeId = found abodeId when the player arrives there now
  - chaosSeaNavigation.discoveredAbodes[] contains found abodeId
- gm_thoughts_markdown:
  - lists the found Guardian as a relevant actor
  - contains a matching `### GuardianName` reasoning block
  - states the search outcome, source = chaos_sea_abode_search, and why this is afterlife Abode navigation rather than Mortal travel
```

Concrete successful-search data points:

```text
Narrative response:
Море Хаоса отвечает на поиск дисциплины без жестокости. Из тумана выходит Обитель Тихого Удара, где Хранитель Каэль принимает душу не как ученика боли, а как ученика равновесия.

Create/update surface:
UpdateGuardians.create with a FULL canonical Guardian object, not the abbreviated notes below.

Required ids and labels:
- guardianId = guard_search_kael_quiet_strike_001
- guardianName / canonicalName = Каэль
- domain = Combat
- abodeId = abode_kael_quiet_strike_001
- abode name/location label = Обитель Тихого Удара
- source/reason = chaos_sea_abode_search

Navigation if the soul actually arrives:
- activeGuardian.guardianId = guard_search_kael_quiet_strike_001
- chaosSeaNavigation.currentAbodeId = abode_kael_quiet_strike_001
- chaosSeaNavigation.discoveredAbodes contains abode_kael_quiet_strike_001
- the created/updated Guardian abode has isDiscovered = true

Reasoning scope:
- Режим: Guardian-centric
- Afterlife subtype: Freeform Chaos Sea Abode search
- Релевантные акторы: Каэль
- Matching block: ### Каэль
- The block explains situation, thoughts, and actions.
```

Player-facing Chaos Sea audit preview checklist:

```text
The GM does not author these previews, but the prompts/examples must teach the same mechanical expectations the player sees before confirming costly or destructive Chaos Sea actions.

Soul Imprint:
- Preview shows Ink Feathers before/after.
- Preview names the companion/NPC source identity: sourceCompanionId or NPCId when known, companionName/NPCName, relationship role, current context.
- GM closure must write a soulImprint entry in game_state/meta/soul_state.json with imprintId, source identity, summary, coreTraits, personalityMarkers, relationshipMarkers, appearanceMotifs, and futureCompanionPrompt.
- output/ink_feather_action_result.json stateEvidence includes stateEvidence.imprintId, stateEvidence.companionName, stateEvidence.affectedFiles, and confirms that no Mortal World NPC was created during the afterlife turn.

Guardian donation/favor:
- Preview shows target Guardian identity and the reputation delta contract.
- Donation uses reputationChange = min(25, max(15, cost / 3)), and the target Guardian's currentReputation must increase by exactly that same amount from the validated pre-turn baseline. A smaller or larger positive delta is still invalid.
- Favor has variable cost but still requires a positive reputation/state result and stateEvidence.guardianId, guardianName, reputationChange, and affectedFiles.

Abode Offering:
- Preview shows consumed object details before confirmation: offeringType, relicId/relicName/relicRarity or archiveId/archiveTitle/archiveEntryType/archiveRarity or inkFeathersOffered.
- Preview shows Abode Power current -> projected, baseDelta, finalDelta, returnCycleId, and remaining offering cap when relevant.
- GM closure writes guardianPowerEvents.reasonType=offering, sourceSurface=guardianAbodeOffering, powerEventId, baseDelta, finalDelta, and the matching journal event.
- Only offeringType=ink_feathers also writes output/ink_feather_action_result.json; relic/archive offerings close through guardianPowerEvents and journal proof only.

Resident notifications:
- Notification detail surfaces must show the resident id/name, current presence, current Guardian/Abode, matching receipt requestId, receipt type, status, responseMode or transferMode, historyEntryId when present, and current bond/devotion/restlessness/migration snapshot.
- The GM must not write afterlife_notifications.json directly; notifications are derived from resident receipts and canonical resident state.

Guardian trade and buyback:
- Guardian trade inventory request previews show pending_guardian_trade_request.json and the GM receipt requirement UpdateGuardianTradeInventoryReceipts.
- Purchase/buyback previews are client-local coordinated writes: no GM turn, but the player sees slotId or buybackEntryId, priceInFeathers, currentFeathers, projectedFeathers, sold-out/buyback status, and full cloned relicData.
- Buyback writes the matching buybackRelics entry from status=available to status=rebought and returns relicData to soulRelics.stored.

Archive consultation/project fuel:
- Preview shows archiveId/title/type/rarity, reservation status, pending request path, and closure through archiveActionResolutions plus soul_state.afterlifeArchive.actionReceipts[].
- lore_fragment may resolve as project_work; secret_record may resolve as pressure_relief only.

Archive candidate manifest:
- `game_state/control/archive_candidate_manifest.json` is client-owned Life Evaluation / archive-candidate state, not a GM pending contract.
- The GM may read it as context or repair evidence, but must not edit, clear, or close it with a receipt.
```

Guardian quest lifecycle through Mortal World:

```text
Baseline Guardian hooks (`questOrigin=guardian_baseline_mortal_life_hook`) are voluntary offers. They stay in availableQuests until the player accepts them.

If the player accepts a Guardian quest before incarnation, it becomes activeQuests[]. During Mortal World play the GM can update only that accepted quest through guardianQuestProgressUpdates[]. This is the narrow exception to the Mortal World "no Guardian mutation" rule.

Use ready_to_turn_in when the mortal objective is satisfied but the soul has not yet returned to the Guardian.

Rare ore example:
- The Guardian asked the player to find a rare ore in the next life.
- The mortal player finds the ore.
- The ore remains in Mortal World inventory/world state. It is not moved into afterlife.
- The Guardian quest receives an itemEcho/memoryImprint/lifeEventEvidence, so the Guardian can later read the imprint from the soul.
```

```json
{
  "guardianQuestProgressUpdates": [
    {
      "guardianId": "guardian_azalia",
      "questId": "quest_azalia_rare_ore_echo",
      "status": "ready_to_turn_in",
      "progressSummary": "Игрок нашёл редкую руду в смертной жизни; физический предмет остался в мире.",
      "readyToTurnInEvidence": {
        "itemEcho": {
          "mortalItemName": "Серебряная руда сна",
          "proofKind": "memory_imprint",
          "summary": "Душа сохранила слепок структуры руды и место находки."
        },
        "lifeEventEvidence": "Событие поиска записано в памяти этой жизни."
      },
      "turnInRequirement": "Вернуться к Хранителю и передать слепок/резонанс, не физический предмет."
    }
  ]
}
```

```text
After the soul returns to the Guardian, close the quest through UpdateGuardians.completeQuest:
```

```json
{
  "UpdateGuardians": [
    {
      "command": "completeQuest",
      "guardianId": "guardian_azalia",
      "questId": "quest_azalia_rare_ore_echo",
      "outcome": "success",
      "questPowerAudit": {
        "questDifficultyTier": "normal",
        "outcome": "success",
        "baseDelta": 3,
        "supportsCurrentProject": false,
        "defendsAgainstRivalPressure": false,
        "bonusDelta": 0,
        "finalDelta": 3
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

Afterlife-origin companion manifestation handoff:

```text
pending_resident_companion_manifestation_request.json is not closable in Chaos Sea or Shining Abode. Valid non-empty files are preserved as next-life context and do not block Soul Gates; malformed files block Soul Gates until repair.
pending_npc_social_interactions.json, pending_npc_trade_inventory_requests.json, and `[NPC_TRADE_REQUEST]` are MortalWorldProfile-only. If any of them exists during Chaos Sea or Shining Abode, treat it as wrong-realm repair-only context: do not close it through npcInteractionJournalUpdates, do not write UpdateNPCs or UpdateNpcTradeInventoryReceipts, and do not mutate game_state/npcs/*.
Valid explicit-empty realm-wrong request files may be client-cleared as stale clutter. Malformed realm-wrong pending files are preserved for repair; do not "fix" them by writing an afterlife receipt or by materializing MortalWorldProfile NPCs.

It can still originate from afterlife resident rewards or imprint-bearing Soul Relics. When it appears later in MortalWorldProfile, the GM must preserve and use:
- requestId
- manifestationSource = resident_relic | imprint_relic
- relicId / relicName
- sourceResidentId when the source is an afterlife resident
- sourceImprintId when the source is an embedded soul imprint
- sourceGuardianId / sourceGuardianName
- targetIncarnation
- companionNameHint
- originWorldSummary
- futureCompanionPrompt
- bondReason

Mortal closure writes an ordinary Mortal NPC/quest path and sets sourceCompanionRelicId plus sourceAfterlifeResidentId or sourceSoulImprintId when applicable.
```

Post-bootstrap Shining blessing effects:

```text
pendingShiningBlessingEffects is runtime-created from preparedIncarnationPackage.selectedCards after Shining bootstrap.
The GM does not author this object during afterlife turns. Most families are consumed later through normal Mortal surfaces:
- memorySelection.status = pending_pre_turn_one_selection -> pre-turn-one memory selection UI/runtime.
- resourceGrant.status = applied_at_bootstrap -> already applied at bootstrap; preserve audit only.
- pendingSocialEffects.status = pending_first_relation_commit -> first qualifying NPC/faction relation commit.
- pendingRouteEffects.status = pending_early_route_seed -> world route event seeds before latestTurn.
- pendingLoreEffects.status = pending_lore_insertion -> world lore event insertion before latestTurn.
- pendingSurvivalEffects.status = pending_first_ruinous_failure -> first ruinous failure mitigation.
- pendingDescentEffects.status = pending_resident_descent -> later companion manifestation/descent closure.
- relicRefinementEntitlements.status = pending_relic_entitlement is the explicit Shining exception: Shining forge previews/requests may consume rerolls/freeShape/freeRetune in Shining Abode. The GM still closes only the resulting pending_shining_abode_actions.json forge request through coreActionReceipts[] and canonical Soul/Shining state; do not author pendingShiningBlessingEffects directly.
- Terminal status consumed -> preserve consumedAtTurn and consumedAtUtc.
- Do not use Terminal status expired for relicRefinementEntitlements. Expired with expiredAtTurn/expiredAtUtc is only for supported route/lore/descent deferred effect arrays.
```

Guardian correction lifecycle and `soul_preparation`:

```text
Guardian project `soul_preparation` is not an immediate reward. It prepares the next Mortal life and is consumed by client-side Guardian correction resolution when the new life starts.

Completed soul_preparation project fields:
- project.projectType = soul_preparation
- project.finalState = Completed
- project.projectOutcomeAudit.preparationBudgetPoints
- project.projectOutcomeAudit.preparationClaimPriorityBonus
- project.effectState.targetIncarnation
- project.effectState.preparationBudgetPointsGranted
- project.effectState.preparationBudgetPointsSpent
- project.effectState.preparationClaimPriorityBonusGranted
- project.effectState.consumedAtLifeStart

Sabotaged soul_preparation project fields:
- project.finalState = Sabotaged
- project.projectOutcomeAudit.hostilePriorityTokensGranted
- project.effectState.targetIncarnation
- project.effectState.hostilePriorityTokensGranted
- project.effectState.hostilePriorityTokensSpent
- project.effectState.consumedAtLifeStart

At Mortal bootstrap the client reads those effectState fields, applies compatible correction claims around next_life_scenario_core.json, writes game_state/world/guardian_corrections.json, consumes the matching effectState budgets/tokens, and may materialize Abode Power spend through guardianPowerEvents.reasonType = correction_spend.

GM rule:
- In Chaos Sea/Shining Abode, never create or edit game_state/world/guardian_corrections.json.
- In Mortal World, read it as client-authored explanation of current-life corrections; do not rewrite the player's scenario core because of it.
- If a correction spend appears in a raw power-event repair/audit context, it uses sourceSurface = guardian_corrections and sourceId = correctionId.
```

Correct reasoning checklist:
- First match the exact tag in `playerAction`.
- If the tag is direct resident reward/quest, do not search for a pending receipt to close.
- If the tag is pending-backed, read the named pending file before writing response state.
- Declare changed residents, Guardians, archive authority, or founded Guardian actors in `gm_thoughts_markdown`.
- Keep all outputs inside afterlife surfaces. Never use Mortal `UpdateNPCs`, Mortal `UpdateQuests`, `worldEventsLog`, or afterlife notifications for these tags.

Why this is valid:
- It separates direct resident player actions from pending resident contracts.
- It gives the GM exact tag names that the client puts into `playerAction`.
- It routes every hidden pending-backed tag to the same canonical surfaces that validators and normalizers expect.

==================================================
EXAMPLE 24: AFTERLIFE SPIRITUAL CONFLICT
==================================================

Scenario:
- `currentRealm = Chaos Sea`.
- A hostile Guardian tries to force the soul toward an unwanted incarnation.
- The player resists. This is not voluntary `TriggerIncarnation`.
- The GM starts an afterlife spiritual conflict instead of writing Mortal combat state.
- Conflict realm/mode is authorized from the validated pre-turn afterlife snapshot. Do not make the start legal by changing `soul_state.currentRealm` or Shining `availability`/`preparedIncarnationPackage` in the same accepted response.
- Russian labels for this system are fixed in `OtherGuides/Afterlife_Combat_Terminology_Glossary.md`: "духовный конфликт посмертия" = `afterlife spiritual conflict`, "обмен действиями" = `exchange`, "аудит кубиков" = `diceAudit`. JSON keys and enum values remain English.

Correct accepted response:

```json
{
  "response": "Лиора не может просто бросить душу во врата. Между вами раскрывается духовный поединок: не смертный бой, а спор воли, связей и права на следующий путь.",
  "gm_thoughts_markdown": "## Охват NPC-анализа\n- Режим / Mode: Mixed\n- Релевантные акторы / Relevant actors: Игрок, Лиора\n- Почему они релевантны / Why they are relevant: Лиора начинает coercive afterlife conflict, игрок сопротивляется forced incarnation.\n- Акторы вне охвата / Actors outside scope: нет\n- Почему они вне охвата / Why outside scope: Все изменяемые акторы входят в конфликт.\n\n### Игрок\n- Текущая локация / Current location: Море Хаоса, перед вратами Лиоры.\n- Ситуация / Current situation: Игрок отказывается от навязанной инкарнации.\n- Мысли / Internal thoughts: Сохранить свободу выбора и не дать Хранителю закрыть спор без поединка.\n- Действия / Intended actions: Сопротивляется давлению и удерживает позицию.\n\n### Лиора\n- Текущая локация / Current location: Море Хаоса, у собственной обители.\n- Ситуация / Current situation: Хранитель пытается продавить guardian_forced incarnation.\n- Мысли / Internal thoughts: Считает, что душа тратит время и должна быть отправлена в жизнь силой.\n- Действия / Intended actions: Начинает духовный конфликт и проверяет сопротивление души.",
  "afterlifeSpiritualConflictUpdate": {
    "mode": "start",
    "conflictSeed": {
      "conflictId": "afterlife_conflict_liora_forced_incarnation_001",
      "realm": "Chaos Sea",
      "sideModel": "direct_duel",
      "status": "active",
      "resolutionState": "active",
      "conflictPosition": "contested",
      "playerSideStrain": "clear",
      "oppositionSideStrain": "clear",
      "stakes": {
        "primary": "Whether Liora can force guardian_forced incarnation without player consent.",
        "voluntaryIncarnation": false,
        "coerciveIncarnationRequiresConflictLoss": true
      },
      "playerSide": {
        "leadContestant": {
          "actorType": "player",
          "actorId": "player_soul",
          "displayName": "Игрок",
          "purchasedArtTierSnapshot": {
            "guard": 1,
            "counter": 1,
            "incarnation_resistance": 1
          }
        },
        "supporters": []
      },
      "oppositionSide": {
        "leadContestant": {
          "actorType": "guardian",
          "actorId": "guardian_liora_001",
          "displayName": "Лиора",
          "actorArtTierSnapshot": {
            "pressure": 3,
            "force_incarnation": 2,
            "binding": 2
          },
          "artAuthoritySource": "guardian_state.relationshipData + abodePower + narrative authority"
        },
        "supporters": []
      },
      "actionEconomy": {
        "player": { "current": 6, "max": 6, "source": "Средоточие Души tier 0" },
        "opposition": { "current": 6, "max": 6, "source": "guardian spiritual authority" }
      },
      "exchangeLog": []
    }
  }
}
```

If the player later sends either the explicit command-shaped action:

```text
[AFTERLIFE_SPIRITUAL_ACTION: afterlife_conflict_liora_forced_incarnation_001] Я не атакую Лиору напрямую. Я укрепляю своё право выбора и пытаюсь сорвать навязываемую связь.
```

or ordinary roleplay prose while this conflict is still active:

```text
Я не атакую Лиору напрямую. Я укрепляю своё право выбора и пытаюсь сорвать навязываемую связь.
```

the GM should write `afterlifeSpiritualConflictUpdate.mode = "exchange"` or `"resolve"`, not Mortal combat fields. The tag is routing help, not a requirement for artistic player input. A contested exchange or contested resolution must include `diceAudit` from `input/turn_request.json.preGeneratedDices1d20`; the GM may interpret the fiction, but cannot decide who wins the contest by preference alone. Every new/current contested exchange with `diceAudit` must include `matchupAudit` (`playerOperation`, `oppositionOperation`, `primaryResolutionLane`, `riskProfile`, `matchupRationale`) so the exchange follows the tactical matrix instead of GM preference. Active conflicts with current exchanges carry afterlife-only ОД in `actionEconomy.player` and `actionEconomy.opposition`; on `mode=start`, player `current/max` comes from client-owned `soul_state.afterlifeCombatProfile.spiritFocusTier` / `Средоточие Души` (`0/1/2/3/4/5` => max ОД `6/7/8/10/12/15`, source `Средоточие Души tier N`). The GM reads `spiritFocusTier` but must not author or mutate it; `/spiritual_arts` upgrades it locally. Every new/current exchange that spends or restores ОД must include `actionCostAudit.player` with `operationType`, `baseCost`, `minCost`, `artTier`, `effectiveCost`, `before`, and `after`, using `effectiveCost = max(minCost, baseCost - artTier)`. `recover_spiritual_power` / Собрать Средоточие restores +3 ОД on success, +2 on partial_success, and only +0..1 when punished by pressure, maneuver, binding, force_binding, or force_incarnation; use `riskProfile=recovery_timing` when rolled. If an active conflict already has active `controlState`, or the current exchange creates/changes active `controlState`, explicitly write both `before.controlState` and `after.controlState`; use `null` or `{ "level": "none" }` for no active control instead of omitting the field. Every exchange with `diceAudit` must include `before.conflictPosition`. If `before.conflictPosition` is not `contested`, record it as exactly one `conflict_position` modifier in `modifierBreakdown` with exact matching `position` (+2 for advantaged, +4 for dominant, on the side that holds the position); do not split, duplicate, blank, omit, or add extra `conflict_position` entries. `contested` means zero `conflict_position` entries. A blocked exchange may keep before/after identical only when `incomingAction` explains what was prevented. Pick one strict Spiritual Art operation: `pressure` changes opposition strain and must not create `controlState`, `guard` safely protects player strain and may block new control but does not remove existing control; even on setback against direct pressure, player strain may worsen by at most one rank, `counter` needs incomingAction and a measured payoff on success/partial_success/countered but cannot create fresh player control from none, `maneuver` changes position and is stopped by pressure/opposing maneuver/active opposition `controlState`; if opposition `controlState.restrictedOperations` lists the attempted operation, that operation cannot succeed until control is answered, `binding` needs advantage/setup and must create or strengthen player `controlState` only after active opposition control is gone, `force_binding` needs strong leverage and at least two distinct `restrictedOperations`, failed binding/force_binding outcomes (`blocked`, `countered`, `setback`) leave `controlState` unchanged on both sides, including player-control rewrites and opposition anti-control deltas, `break_binding` needs binding/coercive context and must weaken/remove/reverse opposition `controlState`; same-level narrowing of opposition `restrictedOperations` counts as weakened `controlState`, while equal/reordered sets do not count, `incarnation_resistance` is only for force_incarnation control and does not clear ordinary binding control, failed incarnation_resistance outcomes leave forced-incarnation `controlState` unchanged, `champion_coordination` is only for champion_duel, and `recover_spiritual_power` is timing-based recovery rather than attack/defense. Active `controlState` uses `level=hindered|bound|locked`, `controllerSide`, `controlId`, `sourceOperation=binding|force_binding|force_incarnation|break_binding|incarnation_resistance|counter|guard|repair`, non-empty `restrictedOperations`, and `summary`; missing/null means no active control for legacy entries, and `sourceOperation` is not a free operation id. The player can inspect the resulting afterlife combat log through `/spiritual_combat_log` / `/журнал_духовного_боя`.

Correct contested exchange response:

```json
{
```

`diceUsed[].sourceIndex` is zero-based and must point at actual values in the current `turn_request.json.preGeneratedDices1d20`. This example starts from `before.conflictPosition = "contested"`, so no `conflict_position` modifier is present. If the player writes only ordinary prose during an active conflict, use the same shape; do not demand that the text begin with `[AFTERLIFE_SPIRITUAL_ACTION: ...]`.

Преимущество / Помеха uses `diceAudit.rollMode.<side>` with `effectiveMode`, `advantageSources[]`, and `disadvantageSources[]`; every consumed d20 is listed in `diceUsed[]` with `selection=selected|discarded`. Преимущество selects the highest die, Помеха selects the lowest die, and critical success/failure uses the selected die only. Встречные Преимущество и Помеха гасятся: both source arrays non-empty means `effectiveMode=normal` and exactly one die is consumed for that side.

If `game_state/core/game_settings.json.difficulty` is readable, every new/current contested `diceAudit` and any matching `rewardAudit` must include `difficultyAudit`. Table: `normal` / Нормальная gives opposition `+0` and reward `100%`; `hard` / Тяжёлая gives opposition `+1` and reward `125%`; `impossible` / Невозможная gives opposition `+2` and reward `150%`. The GM records exactly that `game_difficulty` modifier on the opposition side, never on the player side. The largest difficulty modifier is still below position dominance `+4` and `light_incarnate` lead `+8`, so difficulty raises risk/reward without making dice dominate strategy.

Correct control exchange: after the soul already has `player_advantaged`, `binding` creates measurable player control instead of pretending to be strain or position damage.

```json
{
  "afterlifeSpiritualConflictUpdate": {
    "mode": "exchange",
    "exchange": {
      "exchangeId": "afterlife_conflict_exchange_binding_001",
      "conflictId": "afterlife_conflict_liora_forced_incarnation_001",
      "operationType": "binding",
      "outcome": "success",
      "summary": "Игрок использует полученное преимущество и накладывает мягкие оковы на маршрут Лиоры.",
      "matchupAudit": {
        "playerOperation": "binding",
        "oppositionOperation": "none",
        "primaryResolutionLane": "binding",
        "riskProfile": "control_leverage",
        "matchupRationale": "Игрок уже имеет преимущество позиции, поэтому обмен разрешается как наложение контроля после рычага."
      },
      "before": {
        "conflictPosition": "player_advantaged",
        "playerSideStrain": "clear",
        "oppositionSideStrain": "strained",
        "actionEconomy": {
          "player": { "current": 6, "max": 6, "source": "Средоточие Души tier 0" },
          "opposition": { "current": 6, "max": 6, "source": "guardian spiritual authority" }
        },
        "controlState": {
          "level": "none",
          "controllerSide": null
        }
      },
      "after": {
        "conflictPosition": "player_advantaged",
        "playerSideStrain": "clear",
        "oppositionSideStrain": "strained",
        "actionEconomy": {
          "player": { "current": 4, "max": 6, "source": "Средоточие Души tier 0" },
          "opposition": { "current": 6, "max": 6, "source": "guardian spiritual authority" }
        },
        "controlState": {
          "level": "hindered",
          "controllerSide": "player",
          "controlId": "control_liora_route_hindered_001",
          "sourceOperation": "binding",
          "restrictedOperations": [ "maneuver", "force_incarnation" ],
          "summary": "Лиора стеснена: она не может свободно перестроиться или навязать воплощение, пока не ответит на оковы."
        }
      },
      "actionCostAudit": {
        "player": {
          "operationType": "binding",
          "baseCost": 4,
          "minCost": 2,
          "artTier": 2,
          "effectiveCost": 2,
          "before": 6,
          "after": 4
        }
      },
      "diceAudit": {
        "formulaVersion": "afterlife_spiritual_conflict_v1",
        "diceSource": "input/turn_request.json.preGeneratedDices1d20",
        "diceUsed": [
          { "side": "player", "sourceIndex": 4, "sides": 20, "value": 14 },
          { "side": "opposition", "sourceIndex": 5, "sides": 20, "value": 9 }
        ],
        "playerTotal": 18,
        "oppositionTotal": 11,
        "margin": 7,
        "outcomeBand": "player_success",
        "modifierBreakdown": {
          "player": [
            { "modifierType": "conflict_position", "source": "conflictPosition", "position": "player_advantaged", "value": 2 },
            { "source": "binding art tier", "value": 2 }
          ],
          "opposition": [
            { "source": "guardian resistance", "value": 2 }
          ]
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
```

Correct break-binding exchange: `break_binding` is not a generic attack; it must weaken, remove, or reverse existing opposition control.

```json
{
  "afterlifeSpiritualConflictUpdate": {
    "mode": "exchange",
    "exchange": {
      "exchangeId": "afterlife_conflict_exchange_break_binding_001",
      "conflictId": "afterlife_conflict_liora_forced_incarnation_001",
      "operationType": "break_binding",
      "outcome": "success",
      "summary": "Игрок разрывает часть чужих оков и возвращает себе свободу движения.",
      "incomingAction": {
        "operationType": "binding",
        "actorId": "guard_liora_silver_current_001",
        "summary": "Лиора удерживает душу оковами маршрута."
      },

      },
      "before": {
        "conflictPosition": "contested",
        "playerSideStrain": "strained",
        "oppositionSideStrain": "strained",

        }
      },
      "after": {

        "playerSideStrain": "strained",
        "oppositionSideStrain": "strained",
        "actionEconomy": {
          "player": { "current": 2, "max": 6, "source": "Средоточие Души tier 0" },
          "opposition": { "current": 6, "max": 6, "source": "guardian spiritual authority" }
        },
        "controlState": {
          "level": "hindered",
          "controllerSide": "opposition",
          "controlId": "control_liora_binding_001",
          "sourceOperation": "break_binding",
          "restrictedOperations": [ "maneuver" ],
          "summary": "Оковы ослаблены: душа всё ещё стеснена, но уже не скована полностью."
        }
      },
      "actionCostAudit": {
        "player": {
          "operationType": "break_binding",
          "baseCost": 3,
          "minCost": 1,
          "artTier": 1,
          "effectiveCost": 2,
          "before": 4,
          "after": 2
        }
      },
      "diceAudit": {
        "formulaVersion": "afterlife_spiritual_conflict_v1",
        "diceSource": "input/turn_request.json.preGeneratedDices1d20",
        "diceUsed": [
          { "side": "player", "sourceIndex": 6, "sides": 20, "value": 14 },
          { "side": "opposition", "sourceIndex": 7, "sides": 20, "value": 9 }
        ],
        "playerTotal": 18,
        "oppositionTotal": 14,
        "margin": 4,
        "outcomeBand": "player_success",
        "modifierBreakdown": {
          "player": [
            { "source": "break_binding art tier", "value": 2 },
            { "source": "current Enlightenment rank", "value": 2 }
          ],
          "opposition": [
            { "source": "guardian binding art tier", "value": 2 },
            { "source": "active Guardian Abode pressure", "value": 3 }
          ]
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
```

Natural 20 / natural 1 is bounded critical logic, not permission to narrate impossible scale, and bounded criticals are symmetric. A favorable critical for the player (player natural 20 or opposition natural 1) raises a worse margin result only to ordinary `player_success`; an unfavorable critical for the player (player natural 1 or opposition natural 20) lowers a better margin result only to ordinary `opposition_success`; opposed criticals cancel back to the margin band. If the critical changes the margin-derived band, include `diceAudit.criticalResult`:

```json
{
  "criticalResult": {
    "playerNaturalRoll": 20,
    "oppositionNaturalRoll": 18,
    "marginOutcomeBand": "decisive_opposition_success",
    "normalizedOutcomeBand": "player_success",
    "scaleLimit": "The soul gains a narrow opening; the stronger Guardian is not defeated or bound by this roll alone.",
    "narrativeConstraint": "Resolve as a plausible successful resistance beat, not as total domination."
  }
}
```

If the player wins a contested conflict and the victory is not `repair_cancel`, `no_effect`, voluntary withdrawal/surrender, or pure negotiation, the GM may grant a small validated currency reward. In `Chaos Sea`, reward currency is `ink_feathers` and must be mirrored by `metaStateUpdates.inkFeatherChanges.add`. In ordinary active `Shining Abode`, reward currency is `light_sparks` and must be mirrored by the exact `shining_abode_state.json.lightSparks` increase. Example Chaos Sea victory closure:

```json
{
  "afterlifeSpiritualConflictUpdate": {
    "mode": "resolve",
    "resolution": {
      "mode": "resolve",
      "conflictId": "afterlife_conflict_liora_forced_incarnation_001",
      "realm": "Chaos Sea",
      "sideModel": "direct_duel",
      "resolutionState": "resolved",
      "resolvedAtTurn": 42,
      "guardianId": "guardian_liora_001",
      "operationType": "incarnation_resistance",
      "playerOutcome": "won",
      "diceAudit": {
        "formulaVersion": "afterlife_spiritual_conflict_v1",
        "diceSource": "input/turn_request.json.preGeneratedDices1d20",
        "diceUsed": [
          {
            "side": "player",
            "sourceIndex": 0,
            "sides": 20,
            "value": 14
          },
          {
            "side": "opposition",
            "sourceIndex": 1,
            "sides": 20,
            "value": 9
          }
        ],
        "playerTotal": 18,
        "oppositionTotal": 14,
        "margin": 4,
        "outcomeBand": "player_success",
        "difficultyAudit": {
          "difficulty": "normal",
          "russianLabel": "Нормальная",
          "source": "game_state/core/game_settings.json.difficulty",
          "oppositionModifier": 0,
          "rewardMultiplierPercent": 100
        },
        "modifierBreakdown": {
          "player": [
            {
              "source": "incarnation_resistance art tier",
              "value": 2
            },
            {
              "source": "current Enlightenment rank",
              "value": 2
            }
          ],
          "opposition": [
            {
              "source": "guardian force_incarnation art tier",
              "value": 2
            },
            {
              "source": "active Guardian Abode pressure",
              "value": 3
            }
          ]
        }
      },
      "rewardAudit": {
        "realm": "Chaos Sea",
        "currency": "ink_feathers",
        "baseAmount": 10,
        "opposingLeadStrength": 3,
        "sideModel": "direct_duel",
        "startingConflictPosition": "contested",
        "challengeTier": 3,
        "outcomeMultiplierPercent": 100,
        "riskMultiplierPercent": 100,
        "riskReason": "The conflict started from contested position against a measured Guardian lead.",
        "difficultyAudit": {
          "difficulty": "normal",
          "russianLabel": "Нормальная",
          "source": "game_state/core/game_settings.json.difficulty",
          "oppositionModifier": 0,
          "rewardMultiplierPercent": 100
        },
        "finalAmount": 30,
        "narrativeReason": "The soul won a contested resistance against a Guardian's coercive incarnation pressure."
      },
      "summary": "Игрок выиграл духовный конфликт и сорвал принудительное воплощение."
    }
  },
  "metaStateUpdates": {
    "inkFeatherChanges": {
      "add": 30
    }
  },
  "response": "Связь Лиоры с вратами рвётся. Душа не только сохраняет свободу выбора, но и собирает Чернильные Перья из рассеянного давления конфликта."
}
```

Reward formula is deterministic: Chaos base `10`, Shining base `1`; `challengeTier` comes from opposing lead strength + side model + starting position; `outcomeMultiplierPercent` is `100` for `player_success` and `150` for `decisive_player_success`; risk is `150/125/100/75/50` for `opposition_dominant/opposition_advantaged/contested/player_advantaged/player_dominant`; difficulty reward multiplier is `100/125/150` for `normal`/`hard`/`impossible`; cap is `120` Ink Feathers or `8` Light Sparks. Formula: `finalAmount = clamp(baseAmount * challengeTier * outcomeMultiplierPercent * riskMultiplierPercent * difficultyAudit.rewardMultiplierPercent / 1_000_000, 0, realmCap)`. Do not write reward fields outside `rewardAudit`, do not duplicate a reward for the same `conflictId`, and do not grant XP, Mortal items, Mortal skills, Radiance, or Enlightenment from this conflict reward.

If the conflict ends with Лиора forcing incarnation, the accepted turn must first close the conflict with current-turn proof and only then write the forced trigger:

```json
{
  "afterlifeSpiritualConflictUpdate": {
    "mode": "resolve",
    "resolution": {
      "mode": "resolve",
      "conflictId": "afterlife_conflict_liora_forced_incarnation_001",
      "resolutionState": "resolved",
      "resolvedAtTurn": 42,
      "guardianId": "guardian_liora_001",
      "operationType": "force_incarnation",
      "playerOutcome": "lost",
      "resolutionKind": "player_loss",
      "diceAudit": {
        "formulaVersion": "afterlife_spiritual_conflict_v1",
        "diceSource": "input/turn_request.json.preGeneratedDices1d20",
        "diceUsed": [
          {
            "side": "player",
            "sourceIndex": 2,
            "sides": 20,
            "value": 5
          },
          {
            "side": "opposition",
            "sourceIndex": 3,
            "sides": 20,
            "value": 18
          }
        ],
        "playerTotal": 9,
        "oppositionTotal": 23,
        "margin": -14,
        "outcomeBand": "decisive_opposition_success",
        "modifierBreakdown": {
          "player": [
            {
              "source": "incarnation_resistance art tier",
              "value": 2
            },
            {
              "source": "current Enlightenment rank",
              "value": 2
            }
          ],
          "opposition": [
            {
              "source": "guardian force_incarnation art tier",
              "value": 2
            },
            {
              "source": "active Guardian Abode pressure",
              "value": 3
            }
          ]
        }
      },
      "summary": "Лиора выиграла духовный конфликт о навязанном воплощении; душа не дала добровольного согласия."
    }
  },
  "TriggerIncarnation": {
    "worldDescription": "Новая жизнь, выбранная Лиорой как принудительное следствие конфликта.",
    "characterDescription": "Душа просыпается в новом теле.",
    "circumstances": "Принудительное воплощение после проигранного afterlife spiritual conflict.",
    "source": "guardian_forced",
    "guardianId": "guardian_liora_001",
    "severityBand": "harsh",
    "reason": "Поражение в духовном конфликте о forced incarnation.",
    "provocationSummary": "Нет добровольного согласия; proof находится в recentConflicts[] текущего хода."
  }
}
```

`resolvedAtTurn` must be the current accepted turn, not a future turn and not an old conflict. This conflict proof replaces only the legacy `[GUARDIAN_PROVOCATION]` evidence requirement; Guardian identity, current Abode, negative reputation, return guard, validated snapshot authority, and severity rules still apply from the validated pre-turn Guardian snapshot, not from same-turn `guardians.json` edits. Do not leave `activeConflict.resolutionState = "resolved"` or `"repair_cancelled"`; terminal states must be produced by `mode=resolve` or `mode=repair_cancel`, with `activeConflict=null`.

Why this is valid:
- The GM starts the conflict from roleplay; the client did not pre-create a combat encounter.
- The response uses `afterlifeSpiritualConflictUpdate`, which maps to `game_state/meta/afterlife_spiritual_conflict_state.json`.
- Once `activeConflict` exists, ordinary player prose can advance it; `/spiritual_action` only adds an explicit conflict tag.
- Contested outcomes include `diceAudit` from current turn visible d20 values; afterlife combat is not resolved by GM fiat.
- It distinguishes coercive `guardian_forced` incarnation from voluntary `TriggerIncarnation`.
- If the player wins instead, the optional reward path is audited by `rewardAudit` and exact currency delta, not by arbitrary GM currency edits.
- It uses side-vs-side fields, not root `opponent`, `playerStrain`, or `opponentStrain`.
- It avoids `enemiesData`, `alliesData`, `combat_log_markdown`, HP, energy, XP, Mortal skills, Mortal NPCs, and Mortal world files.

==================================================
EXAMPLE 25: SOURCE OF LIGHT CAPSTONE
==================================================

Scenario:
- `currentRealm = Shining Abode`.
- `shining_abode_state.json.availability = active`.
- `preparedIncarnationPackage = null`.
- `radiance.tier = 4` and `radiance.experience >= 580`.
- No active or malformed afterlife pending/control contract is live.
- The client has created `game_state/control/pending_source_of_light_capstone.json` after the player chose `/source_of_light` / `/источник_света`.
- This is a one-per-soul full-Radiance capstone reward. It is not `pending_shining_abode_actions.json`, not a Shining core action, and not closed through `coreActionReceipts[]`.

Pre-turn pending request:

```json
{
  "game_state/control/pending_source_of_light_capstone.json": {
    "requestId": "source_of_light_capstone:612",
    "createdAtTurn": 612,
    "createdAtUtc": "2026-05-12T08:00:00Z",
    "radianceExperienceAtRequest": 580,
    "radianceTierAtRequest": 4,
    "rewardPassiveId": "light_incarnate",
    "rewardRelicId": "source_of_light_incarnated_light"
  }
}
```

Correct accepted closure narrative:

```json
{
  "response": "Источник Света открывается не как зал и не как врата, а как причина, из которой когда-то родилось первое сияние. Душа проходит через сцену познания начала мироздания и возвращается уже не просто сияющей, а несущей свет внутри себя.",
  "gm_thoughts_markdown": "## Охват NPC-анализа\n- Режим / Mode: Shining Abode\n- Релевантные акторы / Relevant actors: Игрок, Источник Света как Shining capstone locus\n- Почему они релевантны / Why relevant: закрывается pending_source_of_light_capstone.json и выдаются one-per-soul награды light_incarnate / source_of_light_incarnated_light.\n- Акторы вне охвата / Actors outside scope: Shining factions, residents, Guardians\n- Почему вне охвата / Why outside scope: этот capstone не меняет фракции, резидентов или coreActionReceipts[]."
}
```

Correct accepted state fragments:

```json
{
  "game_state/meta/shining_abode_state.json patch": {

      "completed": true,
```

After this reward, any contested afterlife spiritual conflict at or after `grantedAtTurn` must include explicit turn evidence (`exchangeAtTurn`, `resolvedAtTurn`, or `turnNumber`) and account for `light_incarnate` in `diceAudit.modifierBreakdown.player[]`. The modifier is valid only after the full Source of Light closure exists: completed Shining marker, soul passive, and exactly one matching Incarnated Light Soul Relic. Historical conflict logs before `grantedAtTurn` are not rewritten, and pre-unlock or incomplete-closure `light_incarnate` modifiers are invalid. Example player-led coercive action:

```json
{
  "diceAudit": {
    "formulaVersion": "afterlife_spiritual_conflict_v1",
    "diceSource": "input/turn_request.json.preGeneratedDices1d20",
    "diceUsed": [
      {
        "side": "player",
        "sourceIndex": 0,
        "sides": 20,
        "value": 12
      },
      {
        "side": "opposition",
        "sourceIndex": 1,
        "sides": 20,
        "value": 15
      }
    ],
    "playerTotal": 24,
    "oppositionTotal": 20,
    "margin": 4,
    "outcomeBand": "player_success",
    "modifierBreakdown": {
      "player": [
        {
          "passiveId": "light_incarnate",
          "source": "light_incarnate",
          "value": 12,
          "reason": "+8 player lead contestant, +4 coercive operation"
        }
      ],
      "opposition": [
        {
          "source": "opposition art tier",
          "value": 5
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

Correct reasoning checklist:
- The pending file is a direct object, not `requests[]`.
- Validate full Radiance from the pre-turn Shining snapshot: `radiance.tier = 4`, `radiance.experience >= 580`.
- Do not close this from Chaos Sea, sealed Shining, or Shining pending-bootstrap handoff.
- Do not combine it with unresolved Shining core/trade/political/legacy discovery contracts.
- Do not write `coreActionReceipts[]`.
- Grant exactly one `light_incarnate` passive and exactly one `source_of_light_incarnated_light` Soul Relic.
- `source_of_light_incarnated_light` is a Soul Relic, not a Mortal inventory item. It affects Mortal characteristics only through the existing Soul Relic characteristic bonus path when equipped.
- Future afterlife conflict dice from `grantedAtTurn` onward must explicitly include turn evidence and `light_incarnate`; do not leave the bonus implicit in prose, and do not add it to pre-unlock, incomplete-closure, or historical dice audits.

==================================================
EXAMPLE 26 — Профили сущностей посмертия (`afterlife_entity_profiles_v1`, `afterlife_special_art_learning_v1`)
==================================================

Scenario:
- The soul meets a significant afterlife actor in the Chaos Sea.
- The GM must expose a full profile immediately, not hide known mechanical information.
- The canonical file is `game_state/meta/afterlife_entity_profiles.json`.
- The accepted response uses `afterlifeEntityProfileUpdates[]`; the client normalizes it into `profiles[]`.
- Targeted custom state maintenance uses `afterlifeEntityCustomStateChanges[]`; `statesToAddOrUpdate[]` adds/replaces full `customStates` entries and `statesToRemove[]` deletes ended states.
- Special arts require owner identity, `costMultiplierPercent`, `upgradeCost`, and `trainingConditions` when teachable.
- Roleplay training that teaches the player a special art is recorded through `afterlifeSpecialArtLearningReceipts[]`.
- Learned special arts / изученные особые духовные искусства are upgraded by the client through `/spiritual_arts`; GM conflict output may use them only from validated pre-turn authority.
- If the GM does not write `afterlifeEntityProgressionOverrides[]`, the client uses existing `progression_report.json` afterlife/Shining cycle counts to apply deterministic income, one affordable `progressionStrategy.priorityOrder` upgrade, `lastAutoProgressionCycleKey`, and `progressionLedger[]`. Priority entries may be standard art ids, `specialArts[].artId`, `enlightenment`, `radiance`, or `soul_dissipation`.

Valid response fragment:

```json
{
  "response": "Хранитель Зеркал показывает душе своё истинное досье: его запас Перьев, уровень Просветления и известные духовные искусства становятся видимы.",

```

Correct reasoning checklist:
- `actorType + actorId` is the identity key; do not create duplicate profiles for the same entity.
- `standardArts` and `specialArts[].baseOperation` use the canonical spiritual action ids, while player-facing prose may show Russian labels.
- `specialArts[]` is more expensive than the base action: `costMultiplierPercent` must be above 100, `upgradeCost` records higher progression cost, and teachable arts must list `trainingConditions`.
- If a learned art is recognized, write `afterlifeSpecialArtLearningReceipts[]`; do not silently add it to the player without a receipt.
- If a conflict exchange uses a learned special art, include `specialArtAudit` with `effectNote`, and in `actionCostAudit.player` include `specialArtId`, `specialCostMultiplierPercent`, `standardEffectiveCost`, and multiplied `effectiveCost`; `actionCostAudit.player.artTier` must match validated pre-turn authority, not a GM-invented value.
- `customStates[]` is visible in `/afterlife_profiles`; remove ended states through `afterlifeEntityCustomStateChanges[].statesToRemove[]` instead of leaving stale states forever.
- `progressionStrategy.priorityOrder` may target a standard art id, a `specialArts[].artId`, `enlightenment`, `radiance`, or `soul_dissipation`.
- `afterlifeEntityProgressionOverrides[]` may include `specialArtTierDeltas` and `soulDissipationTierDelta` in addition to currency, standard art, and progression experience deltas.
- `progressionLedger[]` is mandatory audit evidence for auto-progression or `afterlifeEntityProgressionOverrides[]`; each cycle must have a clear `cycleKey`, `source`, income/spending, and summary. Do not hand-edit resources without this audit.
- `lastAutoProgressionCycleKey` prevents the same existing afterlife/Shining progression cycle from being applied repeatedly.
- `soulDissipationTier > 0` is a danger warning, not an automatic kill decision. Motives still decide whether the entity attempts final soul dissipation after a victory.
- Final soul death uses resolved conflict proof, not a free narrative string: write `recentConflicts[].soulDissipationProof` with `targetStabilityCoefficient`, `dissipationTier > targetStabilityCoefficient`, victory proof, and `gmMotivation`. If the target is the player, also write `soul_state.terminalGameOver.message`: `Вы мертвы. Ваша душа окончательно развеяна. Загрузите последнее сохранение и попробуйте снова`.
- Do not write Mortal NPC skills, Mortal combat logs, Mortal inventory, or Mortal profile files for afterlife actor progression.
- In Mortal World turns, preserve `game_state/meta/afterlife_entity_profiles.json`; progress updates belong to afterlife turns unless a future matrix row explicitly says otherwise.

==================================================
COMMON INVALID PATTERNS
==================================================

Invalid afterlife response:

```json
{
  "worldEventsLog": [
    {
      "title": "В Море Хаоса начался шторм"
    }
  ],
  "factionDataChanges": [
    {
      "factionId": "shine_faction_glass_light_001",
      "strengthChange": 5
    }
  ],
  "UpdateNPCs": [
    {
      "NPCId": "res_azalia_liora_001",
      "name": "Лиора"
    }
  ],
  "currentLocationData": {
    "locationName": "Сияющая площадь"
  },
  "timeChange": {
    "minutes": 60
  },
  "progression_schedule": {
    "pendingChaosSeaCycles": 0,
    "lastChaosSeaSimulationOrdinal": 99
  }
}
```

Why invalid:
- `worldEventsLog` is Mortal World world progression.
- `factionDataChanges` is Mortal World faction progression.
- `UpdateNPCs` is for regular mortal NPCs, not Guardians or afterlife residents.
- `currentLocationData` is Mortal World location state; afterlife uses Abodes and Shining state.
- `timeChange` is Mortal World time; afterlife scheduler uses `progressionControl` and `progressionProcessingReport`.
- `progression_schedule.json` is client-owned and cannot be advanced by GM output; the GM writes `progressionProcessingReport` / `progression_report.json` instead.
- File-level rule: in Chaos Sea or Shining Abode, any response surface mapped to `game_state/core/player_status.json`, `game_state/player/*`, `game_state/inventory/*`, `game_state/world/*`, `game_state/npcs/*`, `game_state/combat/*`, `game_state/factions/*`, `lore/current_world/*`, Mortal quest files (`game_state/quests/regular_quests.json`, `quest_history.json`, `plot_outline.json`), or Mortal misc files (`game_state/misc/characteristics.json`, `vehicles.json`, `storage_access.json`, `player_interactions.json`) is forbidden unless a future matrix row explicitly says otherwise.

Correct replacement:

```text
Chaos Sea event -> Guardian/Soul/resident state + progressionProcessingReport
Guardian project -> guardianProjectUpdates / completeGuardianProjects
Resident agency -> residentThoughtJournalUpdates / residentInteractionLogUpdates / UpdateGuardianAbodeResidents
Shining civic change -> game_state/meta/shining_abode_state.json
Shining faction change -> Shining faction state inside shining_abode_state.json
Shining trade -> faction.tradeInventory + faction.tradeInventoryReceipts[]
Player-facing notification -> derive from canonical state/receipts; do not author afterlife_notifications.json manually
```
